This is a highly complex, data-dense financial application. Based on your screenshots and requirements, you are essentially building a Personal Finance ERP (Enterprise Resource Planning) system tailored for your specific budgeting methodology, optimized for mobile data entry but with desktop analytical depth.

Here is the detailed breakdown of features, the best technical approach, and what I need from you before providing the code.

Part 1: Feature Identification & Layout
Based on your images (especially the formula bars), here are the features we need to build:

1. Dashboard (Mobile First / Desktop Detailed)

Mobile: A clean, card-based UI showing current month's Balance, Income, Expenses, and Zakat due.

Desktop: The "All Time Totals" view (Image 2). A massive grid showing every category vs. every year (2018-2026). 2026 is live; previous years are static.

Visuals: Donut charts for Category Share %, and a line graph for Income vs Expenses trend.

2. Transaction Engine (200+ rows/month capacity)

Quick Entry: A bottom sheet or modal on mobile to add a transaction (Date, Amount, Currency, Category, Subcategory, Notes).

Bulk Import: CSV/Excel import for historical data. Must skip all current calculations and just insert raw historical data.

Search/Filter: Find transactions by category, date, amount, or type.

3. Budgeting & Bucket System (The Core Logic)

Buckets: Essential, Discretionary, Financial/Obligatory.

Income Auto-Distribution: When Salary is logged, split it by the defined percentages (e.g., 40% Essential, 35% Savings, 15% Financial, 10% Discretionary) into virtual "buckets".

Next Month's Budget: Calculated based on remaining funds in the current month's buckets.

Carry Over: Any unspent budget rolls into Savings for the next month.

4. Currency & Valuation (Multi-Currency)

Fixed Rate: Default MVR/USD rate.

User Configurable: User can change the rate.

Historical Snapshot: Crucial. When a USD transaction is logged, the app stores the MVR equivalent based on the current rate at the time of entry. Changing the rate later must not alter past transactions.

5. Zakat Module

Manual Nisab: User inputs the Nisab threshold manually.

Monthly Calculation: Calculates Zakat on the savings remaining after expenses for that month. (Note: Zakat is usually annual, but you requested monthly calculation based on monthly savings—we will implement a "Zakat Due" tracker).

Tracking: A ledger for Zakat paid vs. Zakat due.

6. Debt Management (No Interest)

Loans: Track principal, total paid, and remaining balance.

No Interest Logic: Calculations are simply Total Debt - Total Paid.

Consolidated View: Total debt remaining across all loans.

7. Data Portability

Export: Generate detailed Excel/CSV files matching your exact sheet structure.

Import: Map historical columns to the new database. Important: Historical data will be flagged as "Archived" and will not trigger live budget calculations or Zakat alerts.

8. Offline-First & Sync

Works without internet. Saves to local IndexedDB.

Syncs to cloud when online.

Part 2: Best Technical Approach
To handle 200 rows/month, offline capability, and complex calculations without breaking, we need a robust stack.

Frontend:

Framework: React (Vite). It handles complex state well.

UI Library: Tailwind CSS + shadcn/ui (for modern, clean components) or Mantine (great for data grids).

Mobile vs Desktop: Use CSS media queries. On mobile, show stacked cards. On desktop, show react-data-grid or ag-grid for that Excel-like feel.

Charts: Recharts or ApexCharts.

Backend / Database (The "Offline First" setup):

Option A (Recommended for simplicity): Supabase (PostgreSQL) + Dexie.js (IndexedDB wrapper).

How it works: The app reads/writes to Dexie (local). A background process syncs Dexie to Supabase. This ensures the app never feels slow and works offline.

Option B (Heavy Local Control): PouchDB + CouchDB.

Data Logic: We will use Zustand or Redux Toolkit for state management.

Key Algorithmic Approach:

Immutability of History: Never store a "Current Rate" variable and multiply it by an old transaction. Instead, the transaction table has columns: Amount_USD, Rate_At_Time, Amount_MVR. When you change the rate in settings, only new transactions use the new rate.

Bucket Calculation: Don't store the bucket balance. Calculate it dynamically: Bucket_Balance = Sum(Income_Allocated_to_Bucket) - Sum(Expenses_from_Bucket).

Here is the revised, locked-in architecture plan based on your answers.

Phase 1: The Core Engine (Data & Logic) — What we build first
1. Database Schema (Dexie.js for Offline)
We need tables that store exact historical values so future rate changes don't affect them.

transactions: id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type (income/expense), is_historical (bool), notes

bucket_settings: id, essential_pct, discretionary_pct, financial_pct, savings_pct (User changeable)

loans: id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes

loan_payments: id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id (Links to expense)

zakat_settings: nisab_threshold, zakat_rate (Fixed at 2.5% but stored for easy reference)

zakat_log: id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid

exchange_rates: id, date_set, rate (To track when rates changed)

2. The Logic Engine (Zustand + Custom Hooks)

Income Distribution: When a transaction of type Income (Salary) is added, the logic calculates:

Essential_allocated = Amount * essential_pct

Discretionary_allocated = Amount * discretionary_pct

Financial_allocated = Amount * financial_pct

Savings_allocated = Amount * savings_pct

Note: These are virtual calculations. We don't store them as transactions; we calculate them on the fly when rendering the dashboard.

Carry Over Logic:

Remaining_Essential = Essential_allocated - Sum(Essential_Expenses)

If Remaining_Essential > 0, it is added to Savings for the next month.

Wait, your instruction: "Any carry over from the previous month goes into savings." So we need a function calculateNextMonthBudget() that takes the remaining from all buckets and adds them to the Savings bucket for the next month.

Zakat Logic (Sharia-compliant):

You said "whatever is eligible in Sharia." This usually means 2.5% of savings that have been held for one lunar year (Hawl). Since you want a monthly calculation, we will calculate a monthly Zakat due based on the savings balance.

Implementation: At the end of each month, Zakatable_Amount = Total_Savings - Total_Debts (if debts are due immediately). If Zakatable_Amount > Nisab, Zakat_Due = Zakatable_Amount * 0.025. If it's less, Zakat_Due = 0. We will log this in zakat_log.

Loan Repayment:

When a loan payment is logged, it creates two records:

A loan_payment record.
An expense transaction in the Personal category (or a dedicated Debt category). This ensures it hits your budget.
Historical Data Import:

A dedicated "Import Mode" flag. When importing, we bypass all budget/Zakat calculations. We just store is_historical: true. The dashboard will ignore these for live calculations but include them in "All Time Totals" and "Average" calculations.

3. The Custom Mapper (Phase 1 UI)

A simple drag-and-drop or dropdown UI where you upload your CSV, and map your columns (e.g., "Column A is Date", "Column B is Amount") to our database fields. This runs once for migration.

Phase 2: The Mobile-First Frontend (UI)
Framework: React + Vite + Tailwind CSS.

Mobile View:

Home: Big cards showing "Remaining Essential", "Remaining Discretionary", "Total Savings", "Zakat Due".

Add Transaction: A floating action button (FAB) opens a bottom sheet. Large input fields for Amount, Dropdowns for Category/Subcategory. A "Currency" toggle that shows the converted MVR amount instantly.

History: A simple list of recent transactions. Swipe to delete/edit.

Desktop View:

Master Sheet: A large data grid (using ag-grid or react-data-grid). Columns for every month, rows for every category. Uses the exact formulas from your screenshots. This is where you do your deep analysis.

Loan Manager: A table showing all loans and a "Log Payment" button.

Settings: Sliders for the bucket percentages, input for Nisab, input for USD rate.

Phase 3: Offline & Sync
Local: Dexie.js handles all reads/writes instantly. The app never waits for the internet.

Sync: When online, a background process (using navigator.onLine and a sync queue) pushes changes to Supabase.

Conflict Resolution: We will use "Last Write Wins" for simplicity. Since you are the only user, this is fine.

File: src/db/database.js
This sets up the offline-first local database using Dexie.js. It stores transactions, settings, loans, and Zakat logs exactly as we discussed.

javascript
import Dexie from 'dexie';

export const db = new Dexie('FinanceERP');

db.version(1).stores({
  // Transactions: store exact amounts and the rate at the time of transaction
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  
  // Settings: Bucket percentages, Nisab, Current Exchange Rate
  settings: 'key',
  
  // Loans: Track total debt
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  
  // Loan Payments: Link to transactions to avoid double counting
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  
  // Zakat Log: Track monthly Zakat due and paid
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  
  // Exchange Rates: History of rate changes
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate'
});

// Initialize default settings if they don't exist
export const initSettings = async () => {
  const count = await db.settings.count();
  if (count === 0) {
    await db.settings.bulkPut([
      { key: 'essential_pct', value: 40 },
      { key: 'discretionary_pct', value: 10 },
      { key: 'financial_pct', value: 15 },
      { key: 'savings_pct', value: 35 },
      { key: 'nisab_threshold', value: 10000 }, // Default MVR
      { key: 'zakat_rate', value: 2.5 },
      { key: 'current_usd_rate', value: 15.42 } // Default MVR rate
    ]);
  }
};
File: src/store/useStore.js
This is the brain of the app. It handles Zustand state management, calculates bucket distributions, handles carry-over, processes loans, and calculates Zakat. It reads/writes to Dexie.

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  isLoading: true,

  // 1. Load data from Dexie
  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    
    // Convert settings array to object
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, isLoading: false });
  },

  // 2. Update Settings (e.g., changing bucket % or USD rate)
  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // 3. Add Transaction (Handles Income Distribution and Loans)
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    // Calculate MVR amount based on current rate
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false, // Live transaction
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    // If it's a loan payment, log it in the loan_payments table
    if (transactionData.category === 'Personal' && transactionData.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment') {
      await db.loan_payments.add({
        loan_id: transactionData.loan_id, // Must pass loan_id in UI
        date: newTransaction.date,
        amount: amount_mvr,
        transaction_id: id
      });
    }

    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  // 4. Calculate Live Buckets for a specific month
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    
    // Filter transactions for the specific month (YYYY-MM)
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    // Calculate Total Income
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    // Calculate Allocated amounts based on current %
    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    // Calculate Expenses per bucket
    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated } // Savings doesn't get spent
    };
  },

  // 5. Calculate Zakat (Monthly)
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    // Total Savings = Total Income - Total Expenses (This month)
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Income').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const totalExpenses = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    
    const savings = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    const nisab = settings.nisab_threshold || 10000;
    const rate = (settings.zakat_rate || 2.5) / 100;

    let zakatDue = 0;
    if (savings >= nisab) {
      zakatDue = savings * rate;
    }

    return { savings, zakatDue };
  }
}));
File: src/App.jsx
This is a basic shell to verify the database and state are working. We will build the full mobile/desktop UI in Phase 2.

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, Wallet, TrendingUp, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

function App() {
  const { loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, calculateZakat, addTransaction } = useStore();
  const [currentMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7)); // YYYY-MM

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(currentMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(currentMonth);

  // Quick test function to add a transaction
  const handleTestAdd = async () => {
    await addTransaction({
      category: 'Personal',
      subcategory: 'Salary',
      amount_original: 5000,
      currency: 'MVR',
      type: 'Income',
      bucket: 'Income'
    });
    alert('Test Income Added! Check the numbers.');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-4 font-sans">
      <header className="mb-6 flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <button onClick={handleTestAdd} className="bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded-full shadow-lg">
          <Plus size={24} />
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* Bucket Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center text-green-600 mb-2">
            <Wallet size={16} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Essential</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Essential.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center text-blue-600 mb-2">
            <TrendingUp size={16} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Discretionary</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Discretionary.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Zakat Card */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center text-orange-600 mb-2">
          <AlertCircle size={16} className="mr-2" />
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due (This Month)</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
      </div>

      {/* Settings Display */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2">Current Settings</h3>
        <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <div>USD Rate: {settings.current_usd_rate}</div>
          <div>Nisab: {settings.nisab_threshold}</div>
          <div>Essential: {settings.essential_pct}%</div>
          <div>Discretionary: {settings.discretionary_pct}%</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

We're doing this locally? Will this be available online later? 
Yes, we are building it locally first, but the architecture is intentionally designed to be deployed online later with zero rewriting.

Here is exactly how the transition works, and why we are doing it this way:

1. Why Local First?
Building locally first allows us to:

Test the complex math (buckets, Zakat, carry-over) without worrying about server costs or network latency.

Ensure the offline database (Dexie/IndexedDB) works perfectly on your phone before we introduce cloud sync.

Fix any logic bugs immediately without dealing with authentication or deployment pipelines.

2. How It Goes Online Later (Phase 3)
The app is a PWA (Progressive Web App). Here is the roadmap to make it online:

Step A: Add a Cloud Database (Supabase)
We will create a free Supabase project (PostgreSQL). We will add a sync engine.

When you add a transaction offline, it saves to your phone's local Dexie database.

When your phone detects an internet connection, it automatically pushes those local changes to Supabase.

When you open the app on your desktop, it pulls the latest data from Supabase.

Step B: Add Authentication
We will add a simple login (Email/Password or Magic Link) via Supabase. This ensures only you can access your financial data.

Step C: Deploy the Frontend (Vercel or Netlify)
You will run a single command (npm run build), drag the folder to Vercel, and it will give you a live URL (e.g., your-finance-app.vercel.app). You can then "Add to Home Screen" on your phone, and it will look and feel exactly like a native app.

3. How the "Offline First" Magic Works
Because we are using Dexie.js (IndexedDB), the app doesn't need the internet to function.

Scenario: You are at a shop with no signal. You open the app and log an expense.

What happens: Dexie saves it to your phone instantly. The UI updates immediately.

Later: You get home, connect to WiFi. The app silently syncs that transaction to the cloud in the background. You don't have to press a "Sync" button.

4. A Critical Note on Data Safety
Right now, your data is only on the browser you are using.

If you clear your browser cache, your local data will be wiped.

Therefore: Before we move to Phase 2, I highly recommend we build the Export to Excel feature first. That way, you can back up your data manually until the cloud sync is ready.

What this means for you right now:
Keep building locally. Test the math. Break it. Tell me what needs fixing. Once you are happy with the core logic, we will plug in the cloud.

This is a very common error with newer versions of Tailwind CSS. The package has split into two parts, and we need to install the new PostCSS plugin and update the config files to use it.

Here is the complete fix. Stop your server first (Ctrl + C), then run these commands in your terminal.

Step 1: Install the new Tailwind PostCSS package
bash
npm install -D @tailwindcss/postcss
Step 2: Overwrite your postcss.config.js file
Run this command to completely replace the file with the new correct syntax:

bash
echo "export default { plugins: { '@tailwindcss/postcss': {}, autoprefixer: {} } }" > postcss.config.js
Step 3: Overwrite your tailwind.config.js file
Tailwind has changed its config format slightly for the new engine. Run this to replace the old one:

bash
cat > tailwind.config.js << 'EOF'
/** @type {import('tailwindcss').Config} */
export default {
  content: [
    "./index.html",
    "./src/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx}",
  ],
  theme: {
    extend: {},
  },
  plugins: [],
}
EOF
Step 4: Update your src/index.css file
Tailwind's new engine requires a different import syntax. Open src/index.css in your editor and replace everything in it with this:

css
@import "tailwindcss";

body {
  background-color: #f3f4f6;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}
(You can also run this command to do it automatically from the terminal:)

bash
cat > src/index.css << 'EOF'
@import "tailwindcss";

body {
  background-color: #f3f4f6;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}
EOF
Step 5: Restart the dev server

Step 1: Update the Store (src/store/useStore.js)
We need a new function that calculates spending per Category for a given month. Copy and paste this entire file to replace the current one:

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  isLoading: true,

  // 1. Load data from Dexie
  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, isLoading: false });
  },

  // 2. Update Settings
  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // 3. Add Transaction
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    if (transactionData.category === 'Personal' && transactionData.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment') {
      await db.loan_payments.add({
        loan_id: transactionData.loan_id,
        date: newTransaction.date,
        amount: amount_mvr,
        transaction_id: id
      });
    }

    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  // 4. Bucket Balances (Existing)
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  // 5. NEW: Category Totals for the Month
  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    // Group by Category
    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) {
        totals[t.category] = 0;
      }
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    // Sort from highest to lowest
    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  // 6. Zakat Calculation (Existing)
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Income').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const totalExpenses = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    
    const savings = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    const nisab = settings.nisab_threshold || 10000;
    const rate = (settings.zakat_rate || 2.5) / 100;

    let zakatDue = 0;
    if (savings >= nisab) {
      zakatDue = savings * rate;
    }

    return { savings, zakatDue };
  }
}));
Step 2: Update the App UI (src/App.jsx)
This is the complete new UI. It includes:

Category totals list (main focus)

Bucket balances (secondary)

A real Add Transaction modal (instead of the test button)

A Month Picker so you can see different months

Copy and paste this entire file:

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, Wallet, TrendingUp, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle } from 'lucide-react';

// List of categories (matching your Excel)
const CATEGORIES = [
  'Personal', 'Family', 'Boalhadhage', 'Income', 'Debt',
  'Donation', 'Electronics', 'Games', 'Medical', 'Loans MVR',
  'Online Shopping', 'Other', 'PC/Accessory', 'Phone/Accessory',
  'Pension', 'Restaurant', 'Shipping Fee', 'Snacks', 'Software',
  'Staff Shop', 'Tickets', 'Transportation', 'Zakat', 'Subscription',
  'Loan Repayment', 'Room Cleaning', 'Samu', 'Hotel Bookings', 'Grocery'
];

const SUB_CATEGORIES = {
  Personal: ['Bills', 'Book', 'Cosmetics', 'Donation', 'Electronics', 'Games', 'Medical', 'Online Shopping', 'Other', 'PC/Accessory', 'Phone/Accessory', 'Pension', 'Restaurant', 'Shipping Fee', 'Snacks', 'Software', 'Staff Shop', 'Subscription', 'Transportation', 'Zakat', 'Loan Repayment', 'Room Cleaning', 'Samu', 'Hotel Bookings', 'Grocery', 'Salary', 'Bonus', 'Other Income'],
  Family: ['Mamma', 'Bappa', 'Dhattha', 'Aiu', 'Ahamadh', 'Faaththu', 'Gifts', 'Toys', 'Rent', 'Combined', 'Food', 'Maintenance', 'Cleaning Goods', 'School Supplies', 'Wardrobe Ads', 'Wardrobe Kids', 'Baby care', 'Waste mngmnt', 'Medical'],
  Boalhadhage: ['Appliances', 'Ceiling / Roofing', 'Cement/Building', 'Furniture', 'Doors', 'General', 'Tiles', 'Wiring', 'Phone/Internet', 'Water', 'Electricity', 'Food'],
  Income: ['Salary/Wages', 'Service charge', 'Refunds/Reimbursements', 'Bonus', 'Misc', 'Savings Fund'],
  Debt: ['Loan', 'Bank Loan']
};

function App() {
  const { loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, calculateZakat, addTransaction } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      {/* Header with Month Picker */}
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <input 
          type="month" 
          value={selectedMonth} 
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
          className="text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none"
        />
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-2">
            <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due ({selectedMonth})</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        {/* Main: Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    {/* Progress Bar background */}
                    <div 
                      className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" 
                      style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
                    ></div>
                    
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Secondary: Buckets */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Essential.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Discretionary.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Financial.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Floating Action Button */}
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {/* Add Transaction Modal */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} 
          onSave={async (data) => {
            await addTransaction(data);
            setIsModalOpen(false);
          }}
          settings={settings}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= MODAL COMPONENT =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings }) {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    type: 'Expense',
    category: 'Personal',
    subcategory: '',
    amount_original: '',
    currency: 'MVR',
    bucket: 'Essential',
    notes: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.amount_original || !formData.category) return;
    
    onSave({
      ...formData,
      amount_original: parseFloat(formData.amount_original)
    });
  };

  const subCats = SUB_CATEGORIES[formData.category] || [];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Add Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          {/* Type Toggle */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFormData({...formData, type: 'Expense'})}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${formData.type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFormData({...formData, type: 'Income'})}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${formData.type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Amount */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={formData.amount_original}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, amount_original: e.target.value})}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={formData.currency}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, currency: e.target.value})}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {formData.currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(formData.amount_original || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Category */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={formData.category}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, category: e.target.value, subcategory: ''})}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {CATEGORIES.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Subcategory */}
          {subCats.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
              <select 
                value={formData.subcategory}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, subcategory: e.target.value})}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="">-- Select --</option>
                {subCats.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Bucket (Only for Expense) */}
          {formData.type === 'Expense' && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bucket</label>
              <select 
                value={formData.bucket}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, bucket: e.target.value})}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="Essential">Essential</option>
                <option value="Discretionary">Discretionary</option>
                <option value="Financial/Obligatory">Financial/Obligatory</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={formData.notes}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, notes: e.target.value})}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit */}
          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            Save Transaction
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

issues; subcats are also in main category drop down. 
i forgot to mention- there should be option to edit/delete previous transactions.

This is very helpful feedback. I see three distinct issues to fix:

Category/Subcategory Separation: Currently my CATEGORIES array is wrong — it's mixing subcategories into the main list. I'll rewrite this to use the exact mapping from your Excel screenshot.

Income Logic: When type = Income is selected, we should ONLY show the Income categories (Salary/Wages, Bonus, etc.), not the full expense category list.

Dropdown "Sticking" Bug: This is a React state management bug. When the user switches types (Income/Expense) or categories, the form keeps stale subcategory values because I used a single formData object. I'll switch to a flat state for cleaner reactivity.

Edit/Delete: We need a recent transactions list with edit/delete actions. This means I need to add new functions to the store (updateTransaction, deleteTransaction) and a list component in App.jsx.

Here is the complete rewrite. Replace both files entirely.

File 1: src/store/useStore.js (Full Replacement)
I've added updateTransaction and deleteTransaction functions, and made sure the addTransaction handles the new structure cleanly.

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  isLoading: true,

  // 1. Load data from Dexie
  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, isLoading: false });
  },

  // 2. Update Settings
  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // 3. Add Transaction
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  // 4. Update Transaction (Edit)
  updateTransaction: async (id, updates) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const existing = get().transactions.find(t => t.id === id);
    if (!existing) return;

    const currentRate = updates.exchange_rate_at_time || existing.exchange_rate_at_time || settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = updates.amount_original !== undefined ? updates.amount_original : existing.amount_original;
    if ((updates.currency || existing.currency) === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = amount_mvr * currentRate;
    }

    const updated = { ...existing, ...updates, amount_mvr, exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate };
    
    await db.transactions.put(updated);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => t.id === id ? updated : t)
    }));
  },

  // 5. Delete Transaction
  deleteTransaction: async (id) => {
    await db.transactions.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id)
    }));
  },

  // 6. Bucket Balances
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  // 7. Category Totals for the Month
  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) totals[t.category] = 0;
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  // 8. Monthly Transactions (for edit/delete list)
  getMonthTransactions: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
  },

  // 9. Zakat Calculation
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Income').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const totalExpenses = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    
    const savings = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    const nisab = settings.nisab_threshold || 10000;
    const rate = (settings.zakat_rate || 2.5) / 100;

    let zakatDue = 0;
    if (savings >= nisab) {
      zakatDue = savings * rate;
    }

    return { savings, zakatDue };
  }
}));
File 2: src/App.jsx (Full Replacement)
This is a big update. Key changes:

CATEGORY_MAP is now a proper object mapping each Category to its exact Subcategories (from your Excel image).

Two dropdowns are dependent on type (Income vs Expense).

Income mode restricts categories to only Income (Salary/Wages, Bonus, Refunds, etc.).

Editing/Deleting: Click any transaction in the "Recent Transactions" list to edit, or swipe/press the trash icon to delete.

Form state is now flat which fixes the "sticking dropdown" issue.

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, Wallet, TrendingUp, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, Trash2, Pencil } from 'lucide-react';

// ============ CATEGORY MAP (from Excel Image 1) ============
const EXPENSE_CATEGORIES = {
  Personal: ['Bills', 'Book', 'Cosmetics', 'Donation', 'Electronics', 'Games', 'Medical', 'Online Shopping', 'Other', 'PC/Accessory', 'Phone/Accessory', 'Pension', 'Restaurant', 'Shipping Fee', 'Snacks', 'Software', 'Staff Shop', 'Subscription', 'Transportation', 'Zakat', 'Loan Repayment', 'Room Cleaning', 'Samu', 'Hotel Bookings', 'Grocery'],
  Family: ['Mamma', 'Bappa', 'Dhattha', 'Aiu', 'Ahamadh', 'Faaththu', 'Gifts', 'Toys', 'Rent', 'Combined', 'Food', 'Maintenance', 'Cleaning Goods', 'School Supplies', 'Wardrobe Ads', 'Wardrobe Kids', 'Baby care', 'Waste mngmnt', 'Medical'],
  Boalhadhage: ['Appliances', 'Ceiling / Roofing', 'Cement/Building', 'Furniture', 'Doors', 'General', 'Tiles', 'Wiring', 'Phone/Internet', 'Water', 'Electricity', 'Food'],
  Debt: ['Loan', 'Bank Loan'],
  Grocery: ['General'],
  Transportation: ['General'],
  Medical: ['General'],
  Education: ['General'],
  'Loan Repayment': ['General'],
  Zakat: ['Monthly'],
  Donation: ['General'],
};

const INCOME_CATEGORIES = {
  Income: ['Salary/Wages', 'Service charge', 'Refunds/Reimbursements', 'Bonus', 'Misc', 'Savings Fund'],
};

function App() {
  const { loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, getMonthTransactions } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <input 
          type="month" 
          value={selectedMonth} 
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
          className="text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none"
        />
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-2">
            <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Essential.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Discretionary.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Financial.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions (Edit/Delete) */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right mr-2">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                    {t.currency === 'USD' && <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">${t.amount_original}</div>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* FAB */}
      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {/* Add/Edit Modal */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= MODAL COMPONENT =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData }) {
  // Flat state — no nested objects. Fixes the dropdown "sticking" bug.
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [bucket, setBucket] = useState(initialData?.bucket || 'Essential');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  // Get the correct category map based on type
  const categoryMap = type === 'Income' ? INCOME_CATEGORIES : EXPENSE_CATEGORIES;
  const availableCategories = Object.keys(categoryMap);
  const availableSubcategories = categoryMap[category] || [];

  // When type switches, reset category/subcategory
  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    const map = newType === 'Income' ? INCOME_CATEGORIES : EXPENSE_CATEGORIES;
    const firstCat = Object.keys(map)[0];
    setCategory(firstCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  // When category switches, reset subcategory
  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount) return;
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: type === 'Expense' ? bucket : 'Income',
      notes,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time // preserve historical rate on edit
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          {/* Type Toggle */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Amount */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Category */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Subcategory */}
          {availableSubcategories.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
              <select 
                value={subcategory}
                onChange={(e) => setSubcategory(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="">-- Select --</option>
                {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Bucket (Only for Expense) */}
          {type === 'Expense' && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bucket</label>
              <select 
                value={bucket}
                onChange={(e) => setBucket(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="Essential">Essential</option>
                <option value="Discretionary">Discretionary</option>
                <option value="Financial/Obligatory">Financial/Obligatory</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit */}
          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

What Changed:
Subcategory Bug Fixed: The dropdown no longer "sticks" on stale values because we use flat state, and any change to type or category resets the subcategory.

Income Mode: Click Income at the top of the modal. The category dropdown now ONLY shows Income, and the subcategory dropdown shows Salary/Wages, Service charge, Refunds/Reimbursements, Bonus, Misc, Savings Fund. No more Personal/Family options in income mode.

Expense Mode: Click Expense. The category dropdown shows Personal, Family, Boalhadhage, Debt, Grocery, Transportation, Medical, Education, Loan Repayment, Zakat, Donation. Each one shows only its correct subcategories based on your Excel "names" sheet.

Recent Transactions List: A new section showing all transactions for the selected month. Each row has:

A pencil icon (edit) — opens the same modal pre-filled, and saving updates the existing transaction.

A trash icon (delete) — asks for confirmation and removes it.

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, Wallet, TrendingUp, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, Trash2, Pencil } from 'lucide-react';

// ============ CATEGORY MAP (Exact match to your CSV) ============
const EXPENSE_CATEGORIES = {
  Personal: [
    'Bills', 'Book', 'Wardrobe', 'Cosmetics', 'Debt', 'Donation', 
    'Electronics', 'Games', 'Medical', 'Loans MVR', 'Loans USD', 
    'Online Shopping', 'Other', 'PC/Accessory', 'Phone/Accessory', 
    'Pension', 'Restaurant', 'Room rent', 'Shipping Fee', 'Snacks', 
    'Software', 'Staff Shop', 'Tickets', 'Transportation', 'Zakat',

  ],
