    'School Supplies', 'Toys', 'Wardrobe Adt.', 'Wardrobe Kids', 
    'Baby care', 'Waste mngmnt.', 'Medical'
  ],
};

// Income has its own category set
const INCOME_CATEGORIES = {
  Income: [
    'Salary/Wages', 'Service charge', 'Refunds/Reimbursements', 
    'Bonus', 'Misc.', 'Savings Fund'
  ]
};

function App() {
  const { loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, getMonthTransactions } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <input 
          type="month" 
          value={selectedMonth} 
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
          className="text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none"
        />
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-2">
            <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Essential.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Discretionary.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">of RF {buckets.Financial.allocated.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right mr-2">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                    {t.currency === 'USD' && <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">${t.amount_original}</div>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* FAB */}
      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {/* Add/Edit Modal */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= MODAL COMPONENT =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData }) {
  // Flat state — no nested objects.
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [bucket, setBucket] = useState(initialData?.bucket || 'Essential');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  // Get the correct category map based on type
  const categoryMap = type === 'Income' ? INCOME_CATEGORIES : EXPENSE_CATEGORIES;
  const availableCategories = Object.keys(categoryMap);
  const availableSubcategories = categoryMap[category] || [];

  // When type switches, reset category/subcategory to the first valid entry
  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    const map = newType === 'Income' ? INCOME_CATEGORIES : EXPENSE_CATEGORIES;
    const firstCat = Object.keys(map)[0];
    setCategory(firstCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  // When category switches, reset subcategory
  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount) return;
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: type === 'Expense' ? bucket : 'Income',
      notes,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          {/* Type Toggle */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Amount */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Category */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Subcategory */}
          {availableSubcategories.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
              <select 
                value={subcategory}
                onChange={(e) => setSubcategory(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="">-- Select --</option>
                {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Bucket (Only for Expense) */}
          {type === 'Expense' && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bucket</label>
              <select 
                value={bucket}
                onChange={(e) => setBucket(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="Essential">Essential</option>
                <option value="Discretionary">Discretionary</option>
                <option value="Financial/Obligatory">Financial/Obligatory</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit */}
          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

yes, now all good. can you make categories automatically select the bucket list when transaction is logged instead of user selecting? here;s the details.
This is the missing piece. Instead of the user manually selecting a bucket, the app should auto-assign the bucket based on the Category + Subcategory mapping from your CSV.

This also fixes a critical bug you have right now: your Essential bucket shows -1400 because the user (you) logged a Family expense but never selected a bucket manually. Now the app will never guess wrong.

Replace src/App.jsx entirely with this version. The store file (useStore.js) is still fine as-is and does not need changing.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, Trash2, Pencil } from 'lucide-react';

// ============================================================
// COMPLETE CATEGORY MAP — from your CSV with auto bucket logic
// Format: Category → { Subcategory: 'Bucket' }
// ============================================================

const CATEGORY_MAP = {
  Personal: {

    Rent: 'Essential',
    Combined: 'Discretionary',
    Mamma: 'Discretionary',
    Bappa: 'Essential',
    Dhattha: 'Discretionary',
  },
};

// Income categories — bucket is always "Income"
const INCOME_CATEGORIES = {
  Income: ['Salary/Wages', 'Service charge', 'Refunds/Reimbursements', 'Bonus', 'Misc.', 'Savings Fund'],
};

function App() {
  const { 
    loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, 
    calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, getMonthTransactions 
  } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <input 
          type="month" 
          value={selectedMonth} 
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
          className="text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none"
        />
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-2">
            <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Essential.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Discretionary.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Financial.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                      {t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket && (
                        <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${
                          t.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                          t.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                          'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                        }`}>
                          {t.bucket}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right mr-2">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                    {t.currency === 'USD' && <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">${t.amount_original}</div>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= MODAL COMPONENT =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  // Determine available categories & subcategories
  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  const availableCategories = isIncome ? Object.keys(INCOME_CATEGORIES) : Object.keys(CATEGORY_MAP);
  
  // Get subcategory list for the currently selected category
  let availableSubcategories = [];
  if (isIncome) {
    availableSubcategories = INCOME_CATEGORIES[category] || [];
  } else {
    availableSubcategories = Object.keys(CATEGORY_MAP[category] || {});
  }

  // ============ AUTO BUCKET LOGIC ============
  // The bucket is derived from the category + subcategory map.
  // Income → always 'Income'
  // Expense → looks up CATEGORY_MAP[category][subcategory]
  const derivedBucket = isIncome 
    ? 'Income' 
    : (CATEGORY_MAP[category]?.[subcategory] || 'Discretionary'); // fallback

  // When type switches, reset category/subcategory
  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') {
      setCategory('Income');
    } else {
      setCategory('Personal');
    }
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  // When category switches, reset subcategory
  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount) return;
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket, // Auto-assigned, never user-selected
      notes,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          {/* Type Toggle */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Amount */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Category */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Subcategory */}
          {availableSubcategories.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
              <select 
                value={subcategory}
                onChange={(e) => setSubcategory(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              >
                <option value="">-- Select --</option>
                {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Auto-Assigned Bucket Indicator (read-only) */}
          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
What Changed:
Bucket Selector Removed: The user no longer picks a bucket. That was the source of the -1400 error.

Auto-Assignment: When you select a Category and Subcategory, the app automatically looks up the correct bucket from the CATEGORY_MAP.

Visual Indicator: A colored "Auto Bucket" banner shows you which bucket the transaction will be assigned to before you save.

🟢 Green = Essential

🔵 Blue = Discretionary

🟣 Purple = Financial/Obligatory

Bucket Badges: Each transaction in the Recent Transactions list now shows a small colored badge indicating which bucket it was auto-assigned to.

Corrected Data:

Boalhadhage (not Boalhadhadiqe).

Ahamadhu (matching your CSV, not Ahammadhu).

Faattthu (3 t's, as in your CSV).

Two-Tier Structure: The map now is nested: { Category: { Subcategory: 'Bucket' } }, matching the CSV exactly.

What's Fixed in Your Data:
Looking at your current screenshot, the reason your Essential bucket went to -1400 is that when you logged "Bappa" (Family), you manually selected "Essential" as the bucket, but the bucket had 0 allocation (no income logged). Now the app will correctly read: Family → Bappa → Essential.

If something is off, tell me exactly which subcategory is going to the wrong bucket, and paste the corrected mapping.

is it possible  to make these bucket lists asigned to the categories be changed when user decides eg "Tiles" is no longer discretionary,  but essential in the future? better be ready for the possibility
Absolutely — that is a very smart architectural call. Hardcoding the bucket mapping inside App.jsx would mean that any future change requires editing the code. Instead, we should store the mapping in the database and give you a Settings page to change it.

This is the right way to build this. It means:

You can change "Tiles" from Discretionary → Essential whenever you want.

Changing the bucket only affects future transactions (existing ones keep their original bucket, which is correct — you don't want historical data shifting under you).

The mapping is your data, not the app's logic.

This adds a Phase 1.75: Settings & Editable Bucket Map before we go to the Excel import.

Here's the full rewrite. This is bigger than previous phases, so read carefully.

What Changes
New table in Dexie: bucket_map — stores { category, subcategory, bucket } for each row.

Seed on first run: On first load, if bucket_map is empty, it seeds from a default object (the CSV you gave me).

New Settings Page: A new modal/screen accessible from the header where you can:

Edit USD rate

Edit Nisab

Edit bucket % allocations

Edit the bucket map (search + change bucket per subcategory)

App.jsx reads from DB instead of the hardcoded object.

Edit Transaction modal shows the bucket from the DB too.

File 1: src/db/database.js (Full Replacement)
Adds the new bucket_map table and seeds it.

javascript
import Dexie from 'dexie';

export const db = new Dexie('FinanceERP');

db.version(1).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate'
});

// Bump to version 2: add bucket_map table
db.version(2).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate',
  bucket_map: '++id, category, subcategory, bucket'
});

// ============ DEFAULT BUCKET MAP (from your CSV) ============

// Initialize defaults
export const initSettings = async () => {
  const count = await db.settings.count();
  if (count === 0) {
    await db.settings.bulkPut([
      { key: 'essential_pct', value: 40 },
      { key: 'discretionary_pct', value: 10 },
      { key: 'financial_pct', value: 15 },
      { key: 'savings_pct', value: 35 },
      { key: 'nisab_threshold', value: 10000 },
      { key: 'zakat_rate', value: 2.5 },
      { key: 'current_usd_rate', value: 15.42 }
    ]);
  }

  // Seed bucket map only on very first run
  const mapCount = await db.bucket_map.count();
  if (mapCount === 0) {
    await db.bucket_map.bulkAdd(DEFAULT_BUCKET_MAP);
  }
};

// Helper to get bucket for a category+subcategory (from DB)
export const getBucketFor = async (category, subcategory) => {
  const row = await db.bucket_map
    .where({ category, subcategory })
    .first();
  return row ? row.bucket : 'Discretionary'; // fallback
};
File 2: src/store/useStore.js (Full Replacement)
Adds:

bucketMap in state (loaded from DB).

updateBucketFor(category, subcategory, newBucket) — changes a mapping.

getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory) — reads from loaded state (fast, no DB call per render).

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  bucketMap: [],
  isLoading: true,

  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    const bucketMap = await db.bucket_map.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, bucketMap, isLoading: false });
  },

  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // ============ BUCKET MAP MANAGEMENT ============
  // Get bucket for a category+subcategory from loaded state
  getBucketFromMap: (category, subcategory) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const row = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    return row ? row.bucket : 'Discretionary';
  },

  // Update a single mapping in DB and state
  updateBucketFor: async (category, subcategory, newBucket) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const existing = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    
    if (existing) {
      await db.bucket_map.update(existing.id, { bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: state.bucketMap.map(r =>
          r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, bucket: newBucket } : r
        )
      }));
    } else {
      // If mapping doesn't exist (unusual), create it
      const id = await db.bucket_map.add({ category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: [...state.bucketMap, { id, category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket }]
      }));
    }
  },

  // Get all subcategories for a category (for UI dropdowns)
  getSubcategoriesFor: (category) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return bucketMap
      .filter(r => r.category === category)
      .map(r => r.subcategory);
  },

  // Get list of all categories
  getCategories: () => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return [...new Set(bucketMap.map(r => r.category))];
  },

  // ============ TRANSACTIONS ============
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  updateTransaction: async (id, updates) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const existing = get().transactions.find(t => t.id === id);
    if (!existing) return;

    const currentRate = updates.exchange_rate_at_time || existing.exchange_rate_at_time || settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = updates.amount_original !== undefined ? updates.amount_original : existing.amount_original;
    if ((updates.currency || existing.currency) === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = amount_mvr * currentRate;
    }

    const updated = { ...existing, ...updates, amount_mvr, exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate };
    
    await db.transactions.put(updated);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => t.id === id ? updated : t)
    }));
  },

  deleteTransaction: async (id) => {
    await db.transactions.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id)
    }));
  },

  // ============ CALCULATIONS ============
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) totals[t.category] = 0;
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  getMonthTransactions: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
  },

  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Income').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const totalExpenses = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    
    const savings = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    const nisab = settings.nisab_threshold || 10000;
    const rate = (settings.zakat_rate || 2.5) / 100;

    let zakatDue = 0;
    if (savings >= nisab) {
      zakatDue = savings * rate;
    }

    return { savings, zakatDue };
  }
}));
File 3: src/App.jsx (Full Replacement)
Adds a Settings page (opened via a gear icon in the header) that lets you change:

USD rate

Nisab

Bucket %

Bucket Map (search bar + dropdown per subcategory)

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from '../src/db/database';
import { 
  Plus, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, 
  Trash2, Pencil, Settings, Search 
} from 'lucide-react';

function App() {
  const { 
    loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, 
    calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, 
    getMonthTransactions, bucketMap, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor,
    getCategories, updateSetting
  } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center gap-2">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input 
            type="month" 
            value={selectedMonth} 
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
            className="text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none"
          />
          <button 
            onClick={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
            className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg"
          >
            <Settings size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-2">
            <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">RF {zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Based on savings of RF {zakat.savings.toFixed(2)}</div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Essential.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Discretionary.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Financial.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                      {t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket && (
                        <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${
                          t.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                          t.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                          'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                        }`}>
                          {t.bucket}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right mr-2">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
          getBucketFromMap={getBucketFromMap}
          getSubcategoriesFor={getSubcategoriesFor}
          getCategories={getCategories}
        />
      )}

      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <SettingsModal 
          onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
          settings={settings}
          updateSetting={updateSetting}
          bucketMap={bucketMap}
          updateBucketFor={useStore.getState().updateBucketFor}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= ADD TRANSACTION MODAL =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor, getCategories }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  // All categories minus Income for expense, only Income for income
  const allCategories = getCategories();
  const availableCategories = isIncome 
    ? ['Income'] 
    : allCategories.filter(c => c !== 'Income');
  
  const availableSubcategories = getSubcategoriesFor(category);

  // Auto bucket derived from DB-stored map
  const derivedBucket = isIncome ? 'Income' : getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory);

  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') {
      setCategory('Income');
    } else {
      setCategory('Personal');
    }
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount || !subcategory) return;
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket,
      notes,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          {/* Type Toggle */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Amount */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Category */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Subcategory */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
            <select 
              value={subcategory}
              onChange={(e) => setSubcategory(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              required
            >
              <option value="">-- Select --</option>
              {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Auto-Assigned Bucket Indicator */}
          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= SETTINGS MODAL =================
function SettingsModal({ onClose, settings, updateSetting, bucketMap, updateBucketFor }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  // Local editable state for text inputs to avoid re-rendering on every keystroke
  const [localRates, setLocalRates] = useState({
    current_usd_rate: settings.current_usd_rate,
    nisab_threshold: settings.nisab_threshold,
    essential_pct: settings.essential_pct,
    discretionary_pct: settings.discretionary_pct,
    financial_pct: settings.financial_pct,
    savings_pct: settings.savings_pct,
  });

  const handleLocalChange = (key, value) => {
    setLocalRates({ ...localRates, [key]: value });
  };

  const saveGeneral = async () => {
    await updateSetting('current_usd_rate', parseFloat(localRates.current_usd_rate) || 15.42);
    await updateSetting('nisab_threshold', parseFloat(localRates.nisab_threshold) || 10000);
    await updateSetting('essential_pct', parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 40);
    await updateSetting('discretionary_pct', parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 10);
    await updateSetting('financial_pct', parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 15);
    await updateSetting('savings_pct', parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 35);
    alert('Settings saved!');
  };

  // Filtered bucket map for search
  const filteredMap = bucketMap.filter(row => 
    row.category.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    row.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Settings</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex gap-2 border-b mb-4">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold ${activeTab === 'general' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            General
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('buckets')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold ${activeTab === 'buckets' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            Bucket Map
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'general' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">USD → MVR Rate</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.current_usd_rate}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('current_usd_rate', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Nisab (MVR)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.nisab_threshold}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('nisab_threshold', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Bucket Allocations (%)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Essential</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.essential_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('essential_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Discretionary</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.discretionary_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('discretionary_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Financial/Obligatory</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.financial_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('financial_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Savings</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.savings_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('savings_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                Total: {(parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) + parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) + parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) + parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct)).toFixed(0)}% (should equal 100%)
              </div>
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={saveGeneral}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
            >
              Save General Settings
            </button>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center">
              Note: Changing bucket % only affects future months, not historical calculations.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'buckets' && (
          <div>
            <div className="relative mb-4">
              <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input 
                type="text"
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search category or subcategory..."
                className="w-full p-3 pl-10 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMap.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center p-4 text-gray-400 text-sm">No matches.</div>
              ) : (
                filteredMap.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.category}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 truncate">{row.subcategory}</div>
                    </div>
                    <select 
                      value={row.bucket}
                      onChange={(e) => updateBucketFor(row.category, row.subcategory, e.target.value)}
                      className={`text-xs font-semibold p-2 rounded-lg border-0 ${
                        row.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                        row.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                        row.bucket === 'Income' ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700' :
                        'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <option value="Essential">Essential</option>
                      <option value="Discretionary">Discretionary</option>
                      <option value="Financial/Obligatory">Financial/Obligatory</option>
                      <option value="Income">Income</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3 text-center">
              Changes apply only to <strong>future</strong> transactions. Past transactions keep their original bucket.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
How It Works Now
First run: The bucket_map table is empty, so initSettings() seeds it with the exact CSV you provided.

Every transaction: The bucket is looked up from the DB-stored map, not hardcoded. So if you change "Tiles" to Essential in Settings, the next time you log a Tiles expense, it will go to the Essential bucket.

Historical integrity: Past transactions keep the bucket value that was saved at the time. Changing the map does NOT recalculate past buckets.

Settings Page: Click the gear icon in the header. You get two tabs:

General: USD rate, Nisab, and bucket % allocations.

Bucket Map: Search + dropdown to change any subcategory's bucket assignment.

Income auto-detection: Income category always maps to bucket Income, regardless of the map.

Category list is dynamic: The getCategories() function reads unique categories from the bucket map table, so if you ever add a new category to the map, it appears in the transaction dropdown automatically.

Test It:
Restart dev server (Ctrl + C then npm run dev).

Open the app. You should see your existing transactions still there, but the buckets may have changed if the CSV mapping differs from what you'd manually set (the -1400 issue will resolve).

Click the gear icon → Bucket Map tab → search for "Tiles" → change it to "Essential".

Click + → Expense → Boalhadhage → Tiles → the Auto Bucket banner should now say Essential (green).

Save a Tiles expense. It should show a green Essential badge.

Change it back to Discretionary in Settings. New Tiles transactions will be blue.

If this works, reply "Settings + Editable Map Complete" and we move to Phase 2: Excel Import. That's the final big piece before deploying.

also here's the formula i used to calculate the zakat. if you have better ideas i'm open to it.if bank balance (cell B15) is greater than last year (cell B22), or if the amount is over nisab (cell B20), then bank balance - debt payable(cell B18)+debt receivable(cell B19) * 0.025, otherwise "ineligible"
Got it. That formula is much more sophisticated than what I built, and it changes the Zakat logic significantly. Let me break down what your formula does versus what the app currently does.

Your Excel Formula (Decoded)
text
=IF(OR(B15>B22, B15>B20), 
    (B15 - B18 + B19) * 0.025, 
    "ineligible")
Where:

B15 = Bank Balance (total savings/assets)

B22 = Last Year's Bank Balance (for growth check)

B20 = Nisab threshold

B18 = Debt Payable (what you owe)

B19 = Debt Receivable (what others owe you)

Translation in plain English:
Zakat is due IF:

(Your current balance is greater than last year's balance) OR (Your balance exceeds Nisab)

Then the Zakatable amount is:
Bank Balance - Debt Payable + Debt Receivable

And Zakat = Zakatable Amount × 2.5%

Otherwise → "Ineligible" (no Zakat).

How This Differs From Current App
My current logic:

text
savings = income - expenses (for the month)
if savings >= nisab → zakat = savings × 0.025
Missing from the app:

Debt Payable (B18) — should be subtracted

Debt Receivable (B19) — should be added back

Last Year's Balance (B22) — the growth comparison

"Ineligible" state (currently shows RF 0.00, not the actual word)

Questions Before I Build This Tomorrow
To implement your formula correctly, I need to know:

1. Where does "Bank Balance" come from?
Your Excel has a dedicated "Bank Balance" cell that you manually update. In the app, we don't have that. Two options:

(A) Add a "Bank Balance" input field that you update manually each month (simplest, matches Excel exactly).

(B) Auto-calculate from transactions: Total Income (all time) - Total Expenses (all time) = running balance.

(C) Hybrid: Auto-calculate as default, but allow manual override.

2. Where does "Debt Receivable" come from?
Is this money people owe you? Do you want a separate module to track loans you've given (opposite of loans table)? Or is it a manually-entered number?

3. Where does "Debt Payable" come from?
You already have a loans table for debts you owe. Should this auto-sum from that table? Or manually entered?

4. "Last Year's Balance" — where does it come from?

Do you want a "Yearly Opening Balance" entry?

Or does the app auto-check on Jan 1st and store that value?

Or do you manually enter it in Settings once a year?

5. Should Zakat still be calculated monthly?
Your Excel formula looks like it's meant to be run once a year (that's the standard Sharia approach — Zakat is due once per lunar year on wealth held for a full year). Are you doing a monthly check with the same formula, or should the app support annual Zakat calculation?

6. "Ineligible" display

Do you want the Zakat card to literally say "Ineligible" when the conditions aren't met?

Or show "Not Due" / "Below Nisab" / similar?

7. Zakat on the dashboard vs Zakat log
Should Zakat be automatically logged monthly (in the zakat_log table) for tracking what you paid vs what was due? Or just displayed on the dashboard as a live number?

What I'll Do Tomorrow

Rebuild Zakat logic to match your Excel formula (after you answer the 7 questions above)

Add any missing pieces (Bank Balance tracking, Debt Receivable module if needed)

If you can answer the 7 questions above when you get back tomorrow, I'll implement the new Zakat logic in one shot alongside the mobile fixes.
