Excellent — this is a much clearer picture. Let me lock in the Zakat redesign before we build it tomorrow.

Confirmed Zakat Logic
Zakatable Amount = Bank Balance - Debt Payable + Debt Receivable

Condition to be eligible:

Bank Balance > Last Year's Same Month Balance OR Bank Balance > Nisab

If eligible: Zakat = Zakatable Amount × 2.5%
If not eligible: Display "Ineligible" (or "Not Due")

Data Sources Confirmed
Component	Source
Bank Balance	Hybrid: Auto-calc from all-time income − all-time expenses, but allow manual override in Settings
Debt Receivable	Manual input in Settings (single value, updated by user)
Debt Payable	Auto-sum from loans table (total_amount − total loan payments made)
Last Year's Monthly Balance	Stored per-month, manually entered OR captured on first load of each month
Nisab	Manual in Settings (already done)
Last Year's Balance — Design Decision Needed
You said: "last year's bal for each month, so that it can be compared to this year's current month."

Two ways to implement:

Option A — Manual entry for all 12 months:
In Settings, a table like:

text
Jan 2025: [___]
Feb 2025: [___]
...
Dec 2025: [___]
You fill these once. App compares Oct 2026's balance to Oct 2025's entry.

Option B — Auto-capture:
On the 1st of each month (or whenever the app is first opened in a month), the app snapshots the bank balance and stores it. Next year, it'll be available for comparison. This means the feature only works after one year of use — you can't compare 2026's October to 2025's October unless you manually enter 2025's October balance.

My recommendation: Option A (manual entry in Settings), with a note that you can also use Option B later once the app has been running for a year. This lets you use the Zakat feature immediately with your existing 2025 data.

Also: Instead of 12 entries, we could make it a table where you enter month-by-month for the previous year only. So if we're in 2026, you'd enter "2025 closing balances per month". When 2027 comes, you'd enter "2026 closing balances per month" (or the app could auto-populate from its own data by then).

Question for tomorrow: Do you want to enter 12 monthly balances for the previous year manually, or just one annual closing balance to compare against? The Excel formula used a single B22 cell, which suggests one number, not 12. Can you clarify?

Zakat Paid Recording
You said: "zakat paid should also be recorded under personal (I believe that's already the case)."

Currently, when you log an expense with Category=Personal, Subcategory=Zakat, it goes into the Financial/Obligatory bucket (from the map). So yes, when you pay Zakat, log it as an expense → it reduces the Financial bucket. The dashboard's "Zakat Due" number is separate (live calculation, not logged).

What I'll add: A dedicated "Mark Zakat as Paid" button on the Zakat card. When clicked, it:

Records a transaction: Personal → Zakat → (amount) with bucket Financial/Obligatory

Logs it in zakat_log for tracking

Reduces the live Zakat Due number by the amount paid

Eligible if Bank Balance > Last Year's Same Month Balance OR Bank Balance > Nisab
Otherwise: "Ineligible"

File 1: src/store/useStore.js (Full Replacement)
New Zakat logic. New state helpers for the settings inputs.

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  bucketMap: [],
  isLoading: true,

  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    const bucketMap = await db.bucket_map.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, bucketMap, isLoading: false });
  },

  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // ============ BUCKET MAP ============
  getBucketFromMap: (category, subcategory) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const row = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    return row ? row.bucket : 'Discretionary';
  },

  updateBucketFor: async (category, subcategory, newBucket) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const existing = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    
    if (existing) {
      await db.bucket_map.update(existing.id, { bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: state.bucketMap.map(r =>
          r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, bucket: newBucket } : r
        )
      }));
    } else {
      const id = await db.bucket_map.add({ category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: [...state.bucketMap, { id, category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket }]
      }));
    }
  },

  getSubcategoriesFor: (category) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return bucketMap
      .filter(r => r.category === category)
      .map(r => r.subcategory);
  },

  getCategories: () => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return [...new Set(bucketMap.map(r => r.category))];
  },

  // ============ LOANS ============
  addLoan: async (loanData) => {
    const id = await db.loans.add(loanData);
    set((state) => ({
      loans: [...state.loans, { ...loanData, id }]
    }));
  },

  getTotalDebtPayable: () => {
    const { loans, transactions } = get();
    const totalLoans = loans.reduce((sum, l) => sum + (l.total_amount || 0), 0);
    
    // Sum all loan payments (logged as "Personal → Loan Repayment")
    const totalRepaid = transactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.category === 'Personal' && t.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return Math.max(0, totalLoans - totalRepaid);
  },

  // ============ TRANSACTIONS ============
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  updateTransaction: async (id, updates) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const existing = get().transactions.find(t => t.id === id);
    if (!existing) return;

    const currentRate = updates.exchange_rate_at_time || existing.exchange_rate_at_time || settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = updates.amount_original !== undefined ? updates.amount_original : existing.amount_original;
    if ((updates.currency || existing.currency) === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = amount_mvr * currentRate;
    }

    const updated = { ...existing, ...updates, amount_mvr, exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate };
    
    await db.transactions.put(updated);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => t.id === id ? updated : t)
    }));
  },

  deleteTransaction: async (id) => {
    await db.transactions.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id)
    }));
  },

  // ============ CALCULATIONS ============
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) totals[t.category] = 0;
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  getMonthTransactions: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
  },

  // ============ NEW ZAKAT LOGIC ============
  // Zakatable Amount = Bank Balance - Debt Payable + Debt Receivable
  // Eligible if: Bank Balance > Last Year's Same Month Balance OR Bank Balance > Nisab
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings, loans } = get();

    // 1. Bank Balance — hybrid: override or auto-calc
    let bankBalance;
    if (settings.bank_balance_override !== undefined && settings.bank_balance_override !== null && settings.bank_balance_override !== '') {
      bankBalance = parseFloat(settings.bank_balance_override);
    } else {
      // Auto-calc: all-time income − all-time expenses
      const totalIncome = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Income' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      const totalExpenses = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      bankBalance = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    }

    // 2. Debt Payable — auto-sum from loans
    const totalLoans = loans.reduce((sum, l) => sum + (l.total_amount || 0), 0);
    const totalRepaid = transactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.category === 'Personal' && t.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const debtPayable = Math.max(0, totalLoans - totalRepaid);

    // 3. Debt Receivable — manual
    const debtReceivable = parseFloat(settings.debt_receivable) || 0;

    // 4. Nisab — manual
    const nisab = parseFloat(settings.nisab_threshold) || 10000;

    // 5. Last year's same month balance — from settings
    // settings.prev_year_balances = { "01": x, "02": y, ..., "12": z }
    const monthKey = monthString.slice(5, 7); // "10" for October
    const prevYearBalances = settings.prev_year_balances || {};
    const lastYearBalance = parseFloat(prevYearBalances[monthKey]) || 0;

    // 6. Eligibility check
    const eligible = bankBalance > lastYearBalance || bankBalance > nisab;

    // 7. Zakatable amount & Zakat
    const zakatableAmount = bankBalance - debtPayable + debtReceivable;
    const zakatDue = eligible && zakatableAmount > 0 ? zakatableAmount * 0.025 : 0;

    return {
      bankBalance,
      debtPayable,
      debtReceivable,
      nisab,
      lastYearBalance,
      zakatableAmount,
      eligible,
      zakatDue
    };
  }
}));

javascript
import Dexie from 'dexie';

export const db = new Dexie('FinanceERP');

db.version(1).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate'
});

db.version(2).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate',
  bucket_map: '++id, category, subcategory, bucket'
});

export const initSettings = async () => {
  const count = await db.settings.count();
  if (count === 0) {
    await db.settings.bulkPut([
      { key: 'essential_pct', value: 40 },
      { key: 'discretionary_pct', value: 10 },
      { key: 'financial_pct', value: 15 },
      { key: 'savings_pct', value: 35 },
      { key: 'nisab_threshold', value: 10000 },
      { key: 'zakat_rate', value: 2.5 },
      { key: 'current_usd_rate', value: 15.42 },
      { key: 'debt_receivable', value: 0 },
      { key: 'bank_balance_override', value: '' },
      { key: 'prev_year_balances', value: {} }
    ]);
  } else {
    // Ensure new keys exist for existing installs
    const existing = await db.settings.toArray();
    const existingKeys = existing.map(s => s.key);
    const defaults = [
      { key: 'debt_receivable', value: 0 },
      { key: 'bank_balance_override', value: '' },
      { key: 'prev_year_balances', value: {} }
    ];
    for (const d of defaults) {
      if (!existingKeys.includes(d.key)) {
        await db.settings.put(d);
      }
    }
  }

  const mapCount = await db.bucket_map.count();
  if (mapCount === 0) {
    await db.bucket_map.bulkAdd(DEFAULT_BUCKET_MAP);
  }
};

jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from '../src/db/database';
import { 
  Plus, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, 
  Trash2, Pencil, Settings, Search, Check, CreditCard, CheckCircle
} from 'lucide-react';

const MONTH_NAMES = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

function App() {
  const { 
    loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, 
    calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, 
    getMonthTransactions, bucketMap, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor,
    getCategories, updateSetting
  } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isZakatPaidOpen, setIsZakatPaidOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkZakatPaid = async (amount) => {
    await addTransaction({
      type: 'Expense',
      category: 'Personal',
      subcategory: 'Zakat',
      amount_original: amount,
      currency: 'MVR',
      bucket: 'Financial/Obligatory',
      notes: `Zakat paid for ${selectedMonth}`,
      date: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    setIsZakatPaidOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans">
      
      {/* Header — responsive: stacks on mobile */}
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-10 p-4 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-center gap-3 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input 
              type="month" 
              value={selectedMonth} 
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedMonth(e.target.value)}
              className="flex-1 sm:flex-initial text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none min-w-0"
            />
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
              className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0"
            >
              <Settings size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="p-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className={`p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6 text-white ${
          zakat.eligible 
            ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400' 
            : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-400 to-gray-500'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <div className="flex items-center">
              <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Zakat Due ({MONTH_NAMES[parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(5,7)) - 1]})</span>
            </div>
            {zakat.eligible && zakat.zakatDue > 0 && (
              <button 
                onClick={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(true)}
                className="text-xs bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/30 px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-semibold flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <Check size={12} /> Mark Paid
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">
            {zakat.eligible ? `RF ${zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}` : 'Ineligible'}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs opacity-80 mt-1 space-y-0.5">
            {zakat.eligible ? (
              <>
                <div>Bank: RF {zakat.bankBalance.toFixed(2)} • Debt Payable: RF {zakat.debtPayable.toFixed(2)} • Receivable: RF {zakat.debtReceivable.toFixed(2)}</div>
                <div>Zakatable: RF {zakat.zakatableAmount.toFixed(2)} × 2.5%</div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div>Bank (RF {zakat.bankBalance.toFixed(2)}) does not exceed Nisab (RF {zakat.nisab.toFixed(2)}) or last year's balance (RF {zakat.lastYearBalance.toFixed(2)})</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Essential.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Discretionary.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">spent RF {buckets.Financial.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500 flex-shrink-0" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm truncate">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                      {t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket && (
                        <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold flex-shrink-0 ${
                          t.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                          t.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                          'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                        }`}>
                          {t.bucket}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 truncate">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right mr-2 flex-shrink-0">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
          getBucketFromMap={getBucketFromMap}
          getSubcategoriesFor={getSubcategoriesFor}
          getCategories={getCategories}
        />
      )}

      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <SettingsModal 
          onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
          settings={settings}
          updateSetting={updateSetting}
          bucketMap={bucketMap}
          updateBucketFor={useStore.getState().updateBucketFor}
        />
      )}

      {isZakatPaidOpen && (
        <ZakatPaidModal 
          onClose={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={handleMarkZakatPaid}
          suggestedAmount={zakat.zakatDue}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= ADD TRANSACTION MODAL =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor, getCategories }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  const allCategories = getCategories();
  const availableCategories = isIncome 
    ? ['Income'] 
    : allCategories.filter(c => c !== 'Income');
  
  const availableSubcategories = getSubcategoriesFor(category);

  const derivedBucket = isIncome ? 'Income' : getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory);

  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') setCategory('Income');
    else setCategory('Personal');
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount || !subcategory) return;
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket,
      notes,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold min-w-0"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
            <select 
              value={subcategory}
              onChange={(e) => setSubcategory(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              required
            >
              <option value="">-- Select --</option>
              {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= ZAKAT PAID MODAL =================
function ZakatPaidModal({ onClose, onConfirm, suggestedAmount }) {
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(suggestedAmount.toFixed(2));

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl p-5">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-600" /> Mark Zakat Paid
          </h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">
          This will record a transaction under <strong>Personal → Zakat</strong> and reduce the Financial bucket.
        </p>

        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount Paid</label>
        <input 
          type="number" 
          step="0.01"
          value={amount}
          onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 mb-4 text-lg font-bold"
        />

        <button 
          onClick={() => onConfirm(parseFloat(amount))}
          className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition"
        >
          Confirm Payment
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= SETTINGS MODAL =================
function SettingsModal({ onClose, settings, updateSetting, bucketMap, updateBucketFor }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  // General tab state
  const [localRates, setLocalRates] = useState({
    current_usd_rate: settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42,
    nisab_threshold: settings.nisab_threshold || 10000,
    essential_pct: settings.essential_pct || 40,
    discretionary_pct: settings.discretionary_pct || 10,
    financial_pct: settings.financial_pct || 15,
    savings_pct: settings.savings_pct || 35,
  });

  // Zakat tab state
  const [zakatInputs, setZakatInputs] = useState({
    debt_receivable: settings.debt_receivable || 0,
    bank_balance_override: settings.bank_balance_override || '',
    prev_year_balances: settings.prev_year_balances || {},
  });

  const handleLocalChange = (key, value) => {
    setLocalRates({ ...localRates, [key]: value });
  };

  const handleZakatInputChange = (key, value) => {
    setZakatInputs({ ...zakatInputs, [key]: value });
  };

  const handlePrevMonthChange = (monthIdx, value) => {
    const key = String(monthIdx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    setZakatInputs({
      ...zakatInputs,
      prev_year_balances: {
        ...zakatInputs.prev_year_balances,
        [key]: value
      }
    });
  };

  const saveGeneral = async () => {
    await updateSetting('current_usd_rate', parseFloat(localRates.current_usd_rate) || 15.42);
    await updateSetting('nisab_threshold', parseFloat(localRates.nisab_threshold) || 10000);
    await updateSetting('essential_pct', parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 40);
    await updateSetting('discretionary_pct', parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 10);
    await updateSetting('financial_pct', parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 15);
    await updateSetting('savings_pct', parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 35);
    alert('General settings saved!');
  };

  const saveZakatInputs = async () => {
    await updateSetting('debt_receivable', parseFloat(zakatInputs.debt_receivable) || 0);
    await updateSetting('bank_balance_override', zakatInputs.bank_balance_override);
    await updateSetting('prev_year_balances', zakatInputs.prev_year_balances);
    alert('Zakat inputs saved!');
  };

  const filteredMap = bucketMap.filter(row => 
    row.category.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    row.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  const totalPct = 
    (parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Settings</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex gap-1 border-b mb-4 overflow-x-auto">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'general' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            General
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('buckets')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'buckets' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            Bucket Map
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('zakat')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'zakat' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            Zakat Inputs
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* ============ GENERAL TAB ============ */}
        {activeTab === 'general' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">USD → MVR Rate</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.current_usd_rate}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('current_usd_rate', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Nisab (MVR)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.nisab_threshold}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('nisab_threshold', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Bucket Allocations (%)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Essential</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.essential_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('essential_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Discretionary</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.discretionary_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('discretionary_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Financial/Obligatory</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.financial_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('financial_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Savings</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={localRates.savings_pct}
                    onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('savings_pct', e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className={`text-xs mt-1 ${totalPct === 100 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                Total: {totalPct.toFixed(0)}% {totalPct !== 100 && '(should equal 100%)'}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={saveGeneral}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
            >
              Save General Settings
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ============ BUCKET MAP TAB ============ */}
        {activeTab === 'buckets' && (
          <div>
            <div className="relative mb-4">
              <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input 
                type="text"
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search category or subcategory..."
                className="w-full p-3 pl-10 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMap.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center p-4 text-gray-400 text-sm">No matches.</div>
              ) : (
                filteredMap.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-2 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.category}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 truncate">{row.subcategory}</div>
                    </div>
                    <select 
                      value={row.bucket}
                      onChange={(e) => updateBucketFor(row.category, row.subcategory, e.target.value)}
                      className={`text-xs font-semibold p-2 rounded-lg border-0 w-full sm:w-auto ${
                        row.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                        row.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                        row.bucket === 'Income' ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700' :
                        'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <option value="Essential">Essential</option>
                      <option value="Discretionary">Discretionary</option>
                      <option value="Financial/Obligatory">Financial/Obligatory</option>
                      <option value="Income">Income</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3 text-center">
              Changes apply only to <strong>future</strong> transactions. Past transactions keep their original bucket.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ============ ZAKAT INPUTS TAB ============ */}
        {activeTab === 'zakat' && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700">
              <strong>Zakat Formula:</strong> (Bank Balance − Debt Payable + Debt Receivable) × 2.5%<br/>
              <strong>Eligible if:</strong> Bank Balance {'>'} Last Year Same Month OR Bank Balance {'>'} Nisab
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Debt Receivable (MVR)</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                value={zakatInputs.debt_receivable}
                onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('debt_receivable', e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                placeholder="Money others owe you"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bank Balance Override (MVR)</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                value={zakatInputs.bank_balance_override}
                onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('bank_balance_override', e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                placeholder="Leave blank to auto-calculate from income − expenses"
              />
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                Leave blank to use auto-calculated value (all-time income − all-time expenses).
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Previous Year Monthly Balances (MVR)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => {
                  const key = String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                  return (
                    <div key={key}>
                      <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{name}</label>
                      <input 
                        type="number"
                        step="0.01"
                        value={zakatInputs.prev_year_balances[key] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handlePrevMonthChange(idx, e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-sm"
                        placeholder="0"
                      />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">
                Compare current month against the same month last year.
              </div>
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={saveZakatInputs}
              className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
            >
              Save Zakat Inputs
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

Dashboard Zakat card: If your bank balance (income − expenses) is greater than 5000 OR greater than Nisab (10000), the card shows the Zakat amount. Otherwise "Ineligible".

Mark Zakat Paid: If eligible, click "Mark Paid" on the Zakat card → confirm → a new transaction appears under Personal → Zakat with the Financial bucket, and the Financial bucket balance drops.

Note: Your screenshot earlier showed RF 12900 savings and RF 322.50 Zakat — that math was 12900 × 0.025. The new logic will only produce that number if Bank Balance > Last Year's October balance OR > Nisab. Otherwise, you'll see "Ineligible".

If everything works, reply "Zakat Rebuild Complete" and we'll move to Phase 2: The Excel Import Mapper — the last big feature before cloud sync.

If you see an error, paste the terminal/browser message here.

Here's the issue. I put 15500 in all months for previous year and when i click mark paid for zakat, there's still another amount shown to be paid this year. I removed all the months income for previous year and kept only October's, same thing still happened. I clicked mark as paid multiple times. Also the date picker and bucket list out of screen issue still there too.
Good — I can see all the issues clearly now. Let me address them one by one.

The Three Problems
1. "Mark as Paid" doesn't reduce the Zakat Due number.

This is a logic design flaw in what I gave you. The "Zakat Due" is a live calculation (bankBalance × 2.5%). Paying Zakat creates an expense, which reduces bankBalance, which reduces the Zakat Due. But because Zakat is 2.5% and the payment is a fraction of the balance, the number barely moves. It never becomes "0" — it just decreases slightly. That's wrong behavior.

Correct approach: Zakat Due should be net of what you've already paid for this month:

text
Zakat Due = (Zakatable Amount × 2.5%) − Sum(Zakat Paid this month)
Once you pay the full amount, Zakat Due = 0 and the card shows "Paid ✓" for the month.

2. Zakat payments are being double-counted in the balance.

Your recent transactions list shows 4 Zakat payments: 298.91, 306.58, 314.44, 322.50. Each one reduced the bank balance. But since each payment is smaller than the total Zakat due, the next calculation shows a slightly lower Zakat Due — which is why they look like they're stacking.

The fix in #1 also fixes this: once you pay, Zakat Due = 0 for that month, and only resets when the month rolls over.

3. Mobile overflow STILL there.

I see it — the date picker and the select dropdowns are STILL overflowing. My previous fix used min-w-0 and flex-1, but the underlying <select> element in Chrome enforces a minimum width based on the longest option. This is a known browser quirk. Real fix: add w-full on the select and constrain its container with max-w-full and overflow-hidden.

Also the date picker — I see it's using <input type="month"> which on Chrome renders a full-width control even when not needed. Fix: put it inside a container with min-w-0 flex-1.

The Complete Fix
I'm rewriting 3 files entirely:

src/store/useStore.js — new Zakat logic with "paid this month" tracking

src/db/database.js — adds zakat_log as a real used table

src/App.jsx — mobile fixes + new Zakat card behavior + paid-state tracking

File 1: src/store/useStore.js (Full Replacement)
New Zakat logic: subtracts Zakat already paid this month.

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  bucketMap: [],
  zakatLog: [],
  isLoading: true,

  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    const bucketMap = await db.bucket_map.toArray();
    const zakatLog = await db.zakat_log.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, bucketMap, zakatLog, isLoading: false });
  },

  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // ============ BUCKET MAP ============
  getBucketFromMap: (category, subcategory) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const row = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    return row ? row.bucket : 'Discretionary';
  },

  updateBucketFor: async (category, subcategory, newBucket) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const existing = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    
    if (existing) {
      await db.bucket_map.update(existing.id, { bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: state.bucketMap.map(r =>
          r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, bucket: newBucket } : r
        )
      }));
    } else {
      const id = await db.bucket_map.add({ category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: [...state.bucketMap, { id, category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket }]
      }));
    }
  },

  getSubcategoriesFor: (category) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return bucketMap
      .filter(r => r.category === category)
      .map(r => r.subcategory);
  },

  getCategories: () => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return [...new Set(bucketMap.map(r => r.category))];
  },

  // ============ TRANSACTIONS ============
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  updateTransaction: async (id, updates) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const existing = get().transactions.find(t => t.id === id);
    if (!existing) return;

    const currentRate = updates.exchange_rate_at_time || existing.exchange_rate_at_time || settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = updates.amount_original !== undefined ? updates.amount_original : existing.amount_original;
    if ((updates.currency || existing.currency) === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = amount_mvr * currentRate;
    }

    const updated = { ...existing, ...updates, amount_mvr, exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate };
    
    await db.transactions.put(updated);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => t.id === id ? updated : t)
    }));
  },

  deleteTransaction: async (id) => {
    await db.transactions.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id)
    }));
  },

  // ============ CALCULATIONS ============
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) totals[t.category] = 0;
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  getMonthTransactions: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
  },

  // ============ ZAKAT LOGIC (fixed) ============
  // Zakatable Amount = Bank Balance - Debt Payable + Debt Receivable
  // Base Zakat = Zakatable Amount × 2.5%
  // Zakat Due = Base Zakat - (Zakat already paid this month)
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings, loans } = get();

    // 1. Bank Balance — override or auto-calc from all-time
    // NOTE: We include ALL live transactions (including zakat payments) so the balance
    //       reflects reality. Then we separately track what's been paid for Zakat.
    let bankBalance;
    if (settings.bank_balance_override !== undefined && settings.bank_balance_override !== null && settings.bank_balance_override !== '') {
      bankBalance = parseFloat(settings.bank_balance_override);
    } else {
      const totalIncome = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Income' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      const totalExpenses = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      bankBalance = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    }

    // 2. Debt Payable
    const totalLoans = loans.reduce((sum, l) => sum + (l.total_amount || 0), 0);
    const totalRepaid = transactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.category === 'Personal' && t.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const debtPayable = Math.max(0, totalLoans - totalRepaid);

    // 3. Debt Receivable
    const debtReceivable = parseFloat(settings.debt_receivable) || 0;

    // 4. Nisab
    const nisab = parseFloat(settings.nisab_threshold) || 10000;

    // 5. Last year's same month
    const monthKey = monthString.slice(5, 7);
    const prevYearBalances = settings.prev_year_balances || {};
    const lastYearBalance = parseFloat(prevYearBalances[monthKey]) || 0;

    // 6. Eligibility
    const eligible = bankBalance > lastYearBalance || bankBalance > nisab;

    // 7. Zakatable amount & BASE zakat
    const zakatableAmount = bankBalance - debtPayable + debtReceivable;
    const baseZakat = eligible && zakatableAmount > 0 ? zakatableAmount * 0.025 : 0;

    // 8. Zakat already PAID this month (via Personal → Zakat expenses)
    const zakatPaidThisMonth = transactions
      .filter(t => 
        t.type === 'Expense' && 
        t.category === 'Personal' && 
        t.subcategory === 'Zakat' && 
        t.date.startsWith(monthString)
      )
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    // 9. Zakat Due = base zakat − already paid
    const zakatDue = Math.max(0, baseZakat - zakatPaidThisMonth);
    const fullyPaid = baseZakat > 0 && zakatDue === 0;

    return {
      bankBalance,
      debtPayable,
      debtReceivable,
      nisab,
      lastYearBalance,
      zakatableAmount,
      baseZakat,
      zakatPaidThisMonth,
      zakatDue,
      eligible,
      fullyPaid
    };
  }
}));
File 2: src/db/database.js (Full Replacement)
No structural changes — just ensuring the zakat_log table exists. Copy the whole file.

javascript
import Dexie from 'dexie';

export const db = new Dexie('FinanceERP');

db.version(1).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate'
});

db.version(2).stores({
  transactions: '++id, date, category, subcategory, amount_original, currency, exchange_rate_at_time, amount_mvr, type, is_historical, notes',
  settings: 'key',
  loans: '++id, name, total_amount, start_date, notes',
  loan_payments: '++id, loan_id, date, amount, transaction_id',
  zakat_log: '++id, month, total_savings, zakat_due, zakat_paid',
  exchange_rates: '++id, date_set, rate',
  bucket_map: '++id, category, subcategory, bucket'
});

const DEFAULT_BUCKET_MAP = [
