export const initSettings = async () => {
  const count = await db.settings.count();
  if (count === 0) {
    await db.settings.bulkPut([
      { key: 'essential_pct', value: 40 },
      { key: 'discretionary_pct', value: 10 },
      { key: 'financial_pct', value: 15 },
      { key: 'savings_pct', value: 35 },
      { key: 'nisab_threshold', value: 10000 },
      { key: 'zakat_rate', value: 2.5 },
      { key: 'current_usd_rate', value: 15.42 },
      { key: 'debt_receivable', value: 0 },
      { key: 'bank_balance_override', value: '' },
      { key: 'prev_year_balances', value: {} }
    ]);
  } else {
    const existing = await db.settings.toArray();
    const existingKeys = existing.map(s => s.key);
    const defaults = [
      { key: 'debt_receivable', value: 0 },
      { key: 'bank_balance_override', value: '' },
      { key: 'prev_year_balances', value: {} }
    ];
    for (const d of defaults) {
      if (!existingKeys.includes(d.key)) {
        await db.settings.put(d);
      }
    }
  }

  const mapCount = await db.bucket_map.count();
  if (mapCount === 0) {
    await db.bucket_map.bulkAdd(DEFAULT_BUCKET_MAP);
  }
};
File 3: src/App.jsx (Full Replacement)
Adds:

Credit card icon in header → opens Loans modal

Loans modal with list + Add/Edit/Delete

Total Debt card on dashboard

"Which loan?" dropdown in Add Transaction when Personal → Loan Repayment is selected

jsx
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from '../src/db/database';
import { 
  Plus, AlertCircle, X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle, 
  Trash2, Pencil, Settings, Search, Check, CheckCircle, 
  ChevronDown, CreditCard, Wallet
} from 'lucide-react';

const MONTH_NAMES = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
const BUCKET_OPTIONS = ['Essential', 'Discretionary', 'Financial/Obligatory', 'Income'];

function useClickOutside(ref, onClose) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target)) onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    document.addEventListener('touchstart', handler);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
      document.removeEventListener('touchstart', handler);
    };
  }, [ref, onClose]);
}

function App() {
  const { 
    loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, 
    calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, 
    getMonthTransactions, bucketMap, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor,
    getCategories, updateSetting, loans, getLoanPaid, getTotalDebtPayable
  } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isZakatPaidOpen, setIsZakatPaidOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLoansOpen, setIsLoansOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);
  const totalDebt = getTotalDebtPayable();

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkZakatPaid = async (amount) => {
    await addTransaction({
      type: 'Expense',
      category: 'Personal',
      subcategory: 'Zakat',
      amount_original: amount,
      currency: 'MVR',
      bucket: 'Financial/Obligatory',
      notes: `Zakat paid for ${selectedMonth}`,
      date: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    setIsZakatPaidOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans overflow-x-hidden">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-30 p-3 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <MonthPicker selectedMonth={selectedMonth} onChange={setSelectedMonth} />
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsLoansOpen(true)}
              className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0 relative"
              title="Loans"
            >
              <CreditCard size={20} />
              {loans.length > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                  {loans.length}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
              className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0"
            >
              <Settings size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="p-3 max-w-4xl mx-auto overflow-hidden">
        
        {/* Zakat Card */}
        <div className={`p-4 rounded-xl shadow-md mb-6 text-white ${
          !zakat.eligible ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-400 to-gray-500' :
          zakat.fullyPaid ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-green-500' :
          'bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-orange-400'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center">
              <AlertCircle size={18} className="mr-2" />
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                Zakat {MONTH_NAMES[parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(5,7)) - 1]} {selectedMonth.slice(0,4)}
              </span>
            </div>
            {zakat.eligible && zakat.zakatDue > 0 && (
              <button 
                onClick={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(true)}
                className="text-xs bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/30 px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-semibold flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <Check size={12} /> Mark Paid
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">
            {!zakat.eligible 
              ? 'Ineligible' 
              : zakat.fullyPaid 
                ? `Paid ✓ (RF ${zakat.baseZakat.toFixed(2)})` 
                : `RF ${zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)}`
            }
          </div>
          
          <div className="text-xs opacity-90 mt-2 space-y-0.5">
            {!zakat.eligible ? (
              <div>Bank before Zakat (RF {zakat.bankBalanceBeforeZakat.toFixed(2)}) does not exceed Nisab (RF {zakat.nisab.toFixed(2)}) or last year's {MONTH_NAMES[parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(5,7)) - 1]} balance (RF {zakat.lastYearBalance.toFixed(2)})</div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div>Bank: RF {zakat.bankBalanceBeforeZakat.toFixed(2)} − Debt: RF {zakat.debtPayable.toFixed(2)} + Receivable: RF {zakat.debtReceivable.toFixed(2)}</div>
                <div>Zakatable: RF {zakat.zakatableAmount.toFixed(2)} × 2.5% = <strong>RF {zakat.baseZakat.toFixed(2)}</strong></div>
                {zakat.zakatPaidThisMonth > 0 && (
                  <div>Paid this month: − RF {zakat.zakatPaidThisMonth.toFixed(2)} {zakat.fullyPaid ? '(fully paid ✓)' : `(remaining: RF ${zakat.zakatDue.toFixed(2)})`}</div>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Total Debt Card */}
        {loans.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm mb-6 border-l-4 border-red-500">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center text-gray-500 mb-1">
                  <Wallet size={14} className="mr-1" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase">Total Debt Remaining</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">RF {totalDebt.toFixed(2)}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">across {loans.length} loan{loans.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</div>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setIsLoansOpen(true)}
                className="text-xs bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg font-semibold text-gray-700"
              >
                Manage
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Category Totals */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">Category Totals</h2>
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">Total: RF {grandTotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          
          {totals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No expenses logged for this month yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {totals.map((item) => {
                const percentage = grandTotal > 0 ? (item.amount / grandTotal) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.category} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 h-full bg-blue-50 opacity-60" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
                    <div className="relative flex justify-between items-center">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">{item.category}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{percentage.toFixed(1)}% of total</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">RF {item.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bucket Balances */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Bucket Balances</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-green-500 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">ESSENTIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">RF {buckets.Essential.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">spent RF {buckets.Essential.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-blue-500 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">DISCRETIONARY</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">RF {buckets.Discretionary.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">spent RF {buckets.Discretionary.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-purple-500 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">FINANCIAL</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">RF {buckets.Financial.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">spent RF {buckets.Financial.spent.toFixed(2)}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-l-4 border-yellow-500 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-semibold">SAVINGS</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">RF {buckets.Savings.remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">allocated</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Transactions */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3">Recent Transactions</h2>
          {monthTransactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center p-6 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm">
              No transactions yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {monthTransactions.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      {t.type === 'Income' 
                        ? <ArrowUpCircle size={16} className="text-green-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                        : <ArrowDownCircle size={16} className="text-red-500 flex-shrink-0" />
                      }
                      <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-sm truncate">{t.subcategory || t.category}</span>
                      {t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket && (
                        <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold flex-shrink-0 ${
                          t.bucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                          t.bucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                          'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                        }`}>
                          {t.bucket}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 truncate">
                      {t.category} • {new Date(t.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                      {t.notes && ` • ${t.notes}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
                    <div className={`font-bold text-sm ${t.type === 'Income' ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-gray-800'}`}>
                      {t.type === 'Income' ? '+' : '-'} RF {t.amount_mvr.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg">
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
          getBucketFromMap={getBucketFromMap}
          getSubcategoriesFor={getSubcategoriesFor}
          getCategories={getCategories}
          loans={loans}
        />
      )}

      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <SettingsModal 
          onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
          settings={settings}
          updateSetting={updateSetting}
          bucketMap={bucketMap}
          updateBucketFor={useStore.getState().updateBucketFor}
        />
      )}

      {isZakatPaidOpen && (
        <ZakatPaidModal 
          onClose={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={handleMarkZakatPaid}
          suggestedAmount={zakat.zakatDue}
        />
      )}

      {isLoansOpen && (
        <LoansModal 
          onClose={() => setIsLoansOpen(false)}
          loans={loans}
          getLoanPaid={getLoanPaid}
          addLoan={useStore.getState().addLoan}
          updateLoan={useStore.getState().updateLoan}
          deleteLoan={useStore.getState().deleteLoan}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= MONTH PICKER =================
function MonthPicker({ selectedMonth, onChange }) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pickerYear, setPickerYear] = useState(parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(0, 4)));
  const ref = useRef(null);
  useClickOutside(ref, () => setIsOpen(false));

  useEffect(() => {
    setPickerYear(parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(0, 4)));
  }, [selectedMonth]);

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative w-full">
      <button 
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="w-full text-sm bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg border-none outline-none text-left flex justify-between items-center"
      >
        <span>
          {MONTH_NAMES[parseInt(selectedMonth.slice(5, 7)) - 1]} {selectedMonth.slice(0, 4)}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown size={16} className={`text-gray-500 transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
      </button>
      
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute z-50 mt-1 w-full bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-gray-200 p-3">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
            <button 
              type="button" 
              onClick={() => setPickerYear(y => y - 1)} 
              className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center"
            >
              ←
            </button>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm">{pickerYear}</span>
            <button 
              type="button" 
              onClick={() => setPickerYear(y => y + 1)} 
              className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center"
            >
              →
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1">
            {MONTH_NAMES.map((m, i) => {
              const monthVal = `${pickerYear}-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
              const isSelected = selectedMonth === monthVal;
              return (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => { onChange(monthVal); setIsOpen(false); }}
                  className={`p-2 text-sm rounded ${
                    isSelected 
                      ? 'bg-blue-500 text-white' 
                      : 'hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {m}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= BUCKET DROPDOWN =================
function BucketDropdown({ currentValue, onSelect }) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef(null);
  useClickOutside(ref, () => setIsOpen(false));

  const colorClasses = (val) => {
    if (val === 'Essential') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
    if (val === 'Discretionary') return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700';
    if (val === 'Income') return 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700';
    return 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700';
  };

  const textColor = (val) => {
    if (val === 'Essential') return 'text-green-700';
    if (val === 'Discretionary') return 'text-blue-700';
    if (val === 'Income') return 'text-yellow-700';
    return 'text-purple-700';
  };

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative w-full">
      <button 
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className={`text-xs font-semibold p-2 rounded-lg border-0 w-full text-left flex justify-between items-center ${colorClasses(currentValue)}`}
      >
        {currentValue}
        <ChevronDown size={14} className={`transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
      </button>
      
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute z-50 mt-1 w-full bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
          {BUCKET_OPTIONS.map(opt => (
            <button
              key={opt}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { onSelect(opt); setIsOpen(false); }}
              className={`w-full text-left p-2 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-gray-100 ${textColor(opt)} ${opt === currentValue ? 'bg-gray-50' : ''}`}
            >
              {opt}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= ADD TRANSACTION MODAL =================
function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor, getCategories, loans }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');
  const [loanId, setLoanId] = useState(initialData?.loan_id || '');

  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  const allCategories = getCategories();
  const availableCategories = isIncome 
    ? ['Income'] 
    : allCategories.filter(c => c !== 'Income');
  
  const availableSubcategories = getSubcategoriesFor(category);

  const derivedBucket = isIncome ? 'Income' : getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory);
  const isLoanRepayment = !isIncome && category === 'Personal' && subcategory === 'Loan Repayment';

  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') setCategory('Income');
    else setCategory('Personal');
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleSubcategoryChange = (newSub) => {
    setSubcategory(newSub);
    if (!(category === 'Personal' && newSub === 'Loan Repayment')) {
      setLoanId('');
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount || !subcategory) return;
    if (isLoanRepayment && !loanId) {
      alert('Please select which loan this repayment is for.');
      return;
    }
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket,
      notes,
      loan_id: isLoanRepayment ? parseInt(loanId) : null,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <select 
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 font-semibold flex-shrink-0"
              >
                <option value="MVR">MVR</option>
                <option value="USD">USD</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <select 
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => handleCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full max-w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
            >
              {availableCategories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
            <select 
              value={subcategory}
              onChange={(e) => handleSubcategoryChange(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full max-w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              required
            >
              <option value="">-- Select --</option>
              {availableSubcategories.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Loan picker — only for Personal → Loan Repayment */}
          {isLoanRepayment && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Which loan?</label>
              {loans.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-xs text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                  No loans yet. Add one via the Loans button in the header first.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <select 
                  value={loanId}
                  onChange={(e) => setLoanId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">-- Select loan --</option>
                  {loans.map(l => (
                    <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name} (RF {l.total_amount.toFixed(2)})</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && !isLoanRepayment && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= ZAKAT PAID MODAL =================
function ZakatPaidModal({ onClose, onConfirm, suggestedAmount }) {
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(suggestedAmount.toFixed(2));

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl p-5">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-600" /> Mark Zakat Paid
          </h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">
          Records a transaction under <strong>Personal → Zakat</strong>. It reduces the Financial bucket and marks this month's Zakat as paid.
        </p>

        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount Paid</label>
        <input 
          type="number" 
          step="0.01"
          value={amount}
          onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 mb-4 text-lg font-bold"
        />

        <button 
          onClick={() => onConfirm(parseFloat(amount))}
          className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition"
        >
          Confirm Payment
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= LOANS MODAL =================
function LoansModal({ onClose, loans, getLoanPaid, addLoan, updateLoan, deleteLoan }) {
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);
  const [editingLoan, setEditingLoan] = useState(null);

  const handleDelete = async (loan) => {
    const paid = getLoanPaid(loan.id);
    const confirmMsg = paid > 0 
      ? `This loan has RF ${paid.toFixed(2)} in recorded payments. Deleting will unlink those transactions but keep them as expenses. Continue?`
      : `Delete loan "${loan.name}"?`;
    if (window.confirm(confirmMsg)) {
      await deleteLoan(loan.id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CreditCard size={20} className="text-red-500" /> Loans
          </h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {!isAdding && !editingLoan && (
          <>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsAdding(true)}
              className="w-full mb-4 bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Plus size={18} /> Add Loan
            </button>

            {loans.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl text-sm">
                No loans yet. Add your first loan above.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {loans.map(loan => {
                  const paid = getLoanPaid(loan.id);
                  const remaining = Math.max(0, loan.total_amount - paid);
                  const pct = loan.total_amount > 0 ? (paid / loan.total_amount) * 100 : 0;
                  return (
                    <div key={loan.id} className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2 gap-2">
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">{loan.name}</div>
                          {loan.start_date && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                              Started {new Date(loan.start_date).toLocaleDateString()}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex gap-1 flex-shrink-0">
                          <button onClick={() => setEditingLoan(loan)} className="p-1.5 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded">
                            <Pencil size={14} />
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => handleDelete(loan)} className="p-1.5 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 rounded">
                            <Trash2 size={14} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs mb-2">
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Total</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">RF {loan.total_amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Paid</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-green-600">RF {paid.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Remaining</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-red-600">RF {remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
                        <div 
                          className="bg-green-500 h-full transition-all"
                          style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, pct)}%` }}
                        ></div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1 text-right">{pct.toFixed(1)}% paid</div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {(isAdding || editingLoan) && (
          <LoanForm 
            initialData={editingLoan}
            onCancel={() => { setIsAdding(false); setEditingLoan(null); }}
            onSave={async (data) => {
              if (editingLoan) {
                await updateLoan(editingLoan.id, data);
              } else {
                await addLoan(data);
              }
              setIsAdding(false);
              setEditingLoan(null);
            }}
          />
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ================= LOAN FORM =================
function LoanForm({ initialData, onCancel, onSave }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialData?.name || '');
  const [totalAmount, setTotalAmount] = useState(initialData?.total_amount || '');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState(
    initialData?.start_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
  );
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name || !totalAmount) return;
    onSave({
      name,
      total_amount: parseFloat(totalAmount),
      start_date: startDate,
      notes
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700">{initialData ? 'Edit Loan' : 'New Loan'}</h3>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Loan Name</label>
        <input 
          type="text"
          required
          value={name}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
          placeholder="e.g., Bank Loan - BML"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Total Amount (MVR)</label>
        <input 
          type="number"
          step="0.01"
          required
          value={totalAmount}
          onChange={(e) => setTotalAmount(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-lg font-bold"
          placeholder="0.00"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Start Date</label>
        <input 
          type="date"
          value={startDate}
          onChange={(e) => setStartDate(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
        <input 
          type="text"
          value={notes}
          onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
          placeholder="Anything to remember"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button 
          type="button"
          onClick={onCancel}
          className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition"
        >
          Cancel
        </button>
        <button 
          type="submit"
          className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
        >
          {initialData ? 'Save Changes' : 'Add Loan'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

// ================= SETTINGS MODAL =================
function SettingsModal({ onClose, settings, updateSetting, bucketMap, updateBucketFor }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  const [localRates, setLocalRates] = useState({
    current_usd_rate: settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42,
    nisab_threshold: settings.nisab_threshold || 10000,
    essential_pct: settings.essential_pct || 40,
    discretionary_pct: settings.discretionary_pct || 10,
    financial_pct: settings.financial_pct || 15,
    savings_pct: settings.savings_pct || 35,
  });

  const [zakatInputs, setZakatInputs] = useState({
    debt_receivable: settings.debt_receivable || 0,
    bank_balance_override: settings.bank_balance_override || '',
    prev_year_balances: settings.prev_year_balances || {},
  });

  const handleLocalChange = (key, value) => {
    setLocalRates({ ...localRates, [key]: value });
  };

  const handleZakatInputChange = (key, value) => {
    setZakatInputs({ ...zakatInputs, [key]: value });
  };

  const handlePrevMonthChange = (monthIdx, value) => {
    const key = String(monthIdx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    setZakatInputs({
      ...zakatInputs,
      prev_year_balances: {
        ...zakatInputs.prev_year_balances,
        [key]: value
      }
    });
  };

  const saveGeneral = async () => {
    await updateSetting('current_usd_rate', parseFloat(localRates.current_usd_rate) || 15.42);
    await updateSetting('nisab_threshold', parseFloat(localRates.nisab_threshold) || 10000);
    await updateSetting('essential_pct', parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 40);
    await updateSetting('discretionary_pct', parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 10);
    await updateSetting('financial_pct', parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 15);
    await updateSetting('savings_pct', parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 35);
    alert('General settings saved!');
  };

  const saveZakatInputs = async () => {
    await updateSetting('debt_receivable', parseFloat(zakatInputs.debt_receivable) || 0);
    await updateSetting('bank_balance_override', zakatInputs.bank_balance_override);
    await updateSetting('prev_year_balances', zakatInputs.prev_year_balances);
    alert('Zakat inputs saved!');
  };

  const filteredMap = bucketMap.filter(row => 
    row.category.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    row.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  const totalPct = 
    (parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Settings</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-1 border-b mb-4 overflow-x-auto">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'general' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            General
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('buckets')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'buckets' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            Bucket Map
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('zakat')}
            className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'zakat' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
          >
            Zakat Inputs
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'general' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">USD → MVR Rate</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.current_usd_rate}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('current_usd_rate', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Nisab (MVR)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  step="0.01"
                  value={localRates.nisab_threshold}
                  onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('nisab_threshold', e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Bucket Allocations (%)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Essential</label>
                  <input type="number" value={localRates.essential_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('essential_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Discretionary</label>
                  <input type="number" value={localRates.discretionary_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('discretionary_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Financial/Obligatory</label>
                  <input type="number" value={localRates.financial_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('financial_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Savings</label>
                  <input type="number" value={localRates.savings_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('savings_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className={`text-xs mt-1 ${totalPct === 100 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                Total: {totalPct.toFixed(0)}% {totalPct !== 100 && '(should equal 100%)'}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveGeneral} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
              Save General Settings
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'buckets' && (
          <div>
            <div className="relative mb-4">
              <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input 
                type="text"
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search category or subcategory..."
                className="w-full p-3 pl-10 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMap.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center p-4 text-gray-400 text-sm">No matches.</div>
              ) : (
                filteredMap.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.category}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 truncate">{row.subcategory}</div>
                    </div>
                    <BucketDropdown 
                      currentValue={row.bucket}
                      onSelect={(newBucket) => updateBucketFor(row.category, row.subcategory, newBucket)}
                    />
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3 text-center">
              Changes apply only to <strong>future</strong> transactions. Past transactions keep their original bucket.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'zakat' && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700">
              <strong>Zakat Formula:</strong> (Bank Balance − Debt Payable + Debt Receivable) × 2.5%<br/>
              <strong>Eligible if:</strong> Bank Balance {'>'} Last Year Same Month OR Bank Balance {'>'} Nisab
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Debt Receivable (MVR)</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                value={zakatInputs.debt_receivable}
                onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('debt_receivable', e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                placeholder="Money others owe you"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bank Balance Override (MVR)</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                value={zakatInputs.bank_balance_override}
                onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('bank_balance_override', e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
                placeholder="Leave blank to auto-calculate"
              />
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                Auto-calc uses all-time income − all-time expenses.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Previous Year Monthly Balances (MVR)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => {
                  const key = String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                  return (
                    <div key={key} className="min-w-0">
                      <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{name}</label>
                      <input 
                        type="number"
                        step="0.01"
                        value={zakatInputs.prev_year_balances[key] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handlePrevMonthChange(idx, e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-sm"
                        placeholder="0"
                      />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveZakatInputs} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
              Save Zakat Inputs
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
How to Use It
Restart dev server.

Tap the new credit card icon in the header → "Add Loan".

Add your first loan:

Name: e.g., "Bank Loan - BML"

Total Amount: e.g., 50000

Start Date: pick the date

Save.

Add the other two loans the same way.

Dashboard now shows a red "Total Debt Remaining" card below the Zakat card.

Log a repayment:

Tap + → Expense → Personal → Loan Repayment.

A new dropdown appears: "Which loan?" → pick the loan.

Enter amount → Save.

The Zakat card's debt number updates instantly, and the loan's progress bar moves.

Edit / Delete loans via the Loans modal (pencil / trash icons).

Zakat math: Debt Payable now comes from the per-loan paid amounts, matching your original Excel formula.

Test Cases
Add a loan of 50,000 → Total Debt = 50,000.

Log a repayment of 5,000 → Loan shows Paid: 5,000 / Remaining: 45,000. Total Debt card shows 45,000.

Zakat card's "Debt: RF ..." line should reflect 45,000, reducing the Zakatable amount.

Try logging a Loan Repayment without selecting a loan → it should alert you to pick one.

Let me know how it goes. If it works, we move to the big one: Excel Import Mapper.
