File 11: src/components/AddTransactionModal.jsx (NEW — uses CustomSelect)
jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { CustomSelect } from './CustomSelect';

export function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor, getCategories, loans }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');
  const [loanId, setLoanId] = useState(initialData?.loan_id || '');

  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  const allCategories = getCategories();
  const availableCategories = isIncome 
    ? ['Income'] 
    : allCategories.filter(c => c !== 'Income');
  
  const availableSubcategories = getSubcategoriesFor(category);

  const derivedBucket = isIncome ? 'Income' : getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory);
  const isLoanRepayment = !isIncome && category === 'Personal' && subcategory === 'Loan Repayment';

  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') setCategory('Income');
    else setCategory('Personal');
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleSubcategoryChange = (newSub) => {
    setSubcategory(newSub);
    if (!(category === 'Personal' && newSub === 'Loan Repayment')) {
      setLoanId('');
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount || !subcategory) return;
    if (isLoanRepayment && !loanId) {
      alert('Please select which loan this repayment is for.');
      return;
    }
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket,
      notes,
      loan_id: isLoanRepayment ? parseInt(loanId) : null,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  const loanOptions = loans.map(l => `${l.id}::${l.name} (RF ${l.total_amount.toFixed(2)})`);
  const loanDisplay = loans.map(l => ({
    id: l.id,
    label: `${l.name} (RF ${l.total_amount.toFixed(2)})`
  }));
  const selectedLoanLabel = loanDisplay.find(l => String(l.id) === String(loanId))?.label || '';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <CustomSelect 
                value={currency}
                onChange={setCurrency}
                options={['MVR', 'USD']}
                searchable={false}
                required={true}
                className="w-24 flex-shrink-0"
              />
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <CustomSelect 
              value={category}
              onChange={handleCategoryChange}
              options={availableCategories}
              required={true}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
            <CustomSelect 
              value={subcategory}
              onChange={handleSubcategoryChange}
              options={availableSubcategories}
              placeholder="-- Select --"
              required={true}
            />
          </div>

          {isLoanRepayment && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Which loan?</label>
              {loans.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-xs text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                  No loans yet. Add one via the Loans button in the header first.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <CustomSelect 
                  value={selectedLoanLabel}
                  onChange={(label) => {
                    const found = loanDisplay.find(l => l.label === label);
                    setLoanId(found ? found.id : '');
                  }}
                  options={loanDisplay.map(l => l.label)}
                  placeholder="-- Select loan --"
                  required={true}
                  searchable={false}
                />
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && !isLoanRepayment && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';

export function ZakatPaidModal({ onClose, onConfirm, suggestedAmount }) {

          Confirm Payment

File 13: src/components/LoanForm.jsx (NEW)
jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { DatePicker } from './DatePicker';

export function LoanForm({ initialData, onCancel, onSave }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialData?.name || '');
  const [totalAmount, setTotalAmount] = useState(initialData?.total_amount || '');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState(
    initialData?.start_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
  );
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name || !totalAmount) return;
    onSave({
      name,
      total_amount: parseFloat(totalAmount),
      start_date: startDate,
      notes
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700">{initialData ? 'Edit Loan' : 'New Loan'}</h3>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Loan Name</label>
        <input 
          type="text"
          required
          value={name}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
          placeholder="e.g., Bank Loan - BML"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Total Amount (MVR)</label>
        <input 
          type="number"
          step="0.01"
          required
          value={totalAmount}
          onChange={(e) => setTotalAmount(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-lg font-bold"
          placeholder="0.00"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Start Date</label>
        <DatePicker value={startDate} onChange={setStartDate} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
        <input 
          type="text"
          value={notes}
          onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
          placeholder="Anything to remember"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button 
          type="button"
          onClick={onCancel}
          className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition"
        >
          Cancel
        </button>
        <button 
          type="submit"
          className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
        >
          {initialData ? 'Save Changes' : 'Add Loan'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
File 14: src/components/LoansModal.jsx (NEW)
jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import { LoanForm } from './LoanForm';

export function LoansModal({ onClose, loans, getLoanPaid, addLoan, updateLoan, deleteLoan }) {
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);
  const [editingLoan, setEditingLoan] = useState(null);

  const handleDelete = async (loan) => {
    const paid = getLoanPaid(loan.id);
    const confirmMsg = paid > 0 
      ? `This loan has RF ${paid.toFixed(2)} in recorded payments. Deleting will unlink those transactions but keep them as expenses. Continue?`
      : `Delete loan "${loan.name}"?`;
    if (window.confirm(confirmMsg)) {
      await deleteLoan(loan.id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CreditCard size={20} className="text-red-500" /> Loans
          </h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {!isAdding && !editingLoan && (
          <>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsAdding(true)}
              className="w-full mb-4 bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Plus size={18} /> Add Loan
            </button>

            {loans.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl text-sm">
                No loans yet. Add your first loan above.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {loans.map(loan => {
                  const paid = getLoanPaid(loan.id);
                  const remaining = Math.max(0, loan.total_amount - paid);
                  const pct = loan.total_amount > 0 ? (paid / loan.total_amount) * 100 : 0;
                  return (
                    <div key={loan.id} className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2 gap-2">
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">{loan.name}</div>
                          {loan.start_date && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                              Started {new Date(loan.start_date).toLocaleDateString()}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex gap-1 flex-shrink-0">
                          <button onClick={() => setEditingLoan(loan)} className="p-1.5 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded">
                            <Pencil size={14} />
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => handleDelete(loan)} className="p-1.5 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 rounded">
                            <Trash2 size={14} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs mb-2">
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Total</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-gray-700">RF {loan.total_amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Paid</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-green-600">RF {paid.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-gray-400">Remaining</div>
                          <div className="font-semibold text-red-600">RF {remaining.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
                        <div 
                          className="bg-green-500 h-full transition-all"
                          style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, pct)}%` }}
                        ></div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1 text-right">{pct.toFixed(1)}% paid</div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {(isAdding || editingLoan) && (
          <LoanForm 
            initialData={editingLoan}
            onCancel={() => { setIsAdding(false); setEditingLoan(null); }}
            onSave={async (data) => {
              if (editingLoan) {
                await updateLoan(editingLoan.id, data);
              } else {
                await addLoan(data);
              }
              setIsAdding(false);
              setEditingLoan(null);
            }}
          />
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
File 15: src/components/SettingsModal.jsx (NEW)
jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { BucketDropdown } from './BucketDropdown';

const MONTH_NAMES = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

export function SettingsModal({ onClose, settings, updateSetting, bucketMap, updateBucketFor }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  const [localRates, setLocalRates] = useState({
    current_usd_rate: settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42,
    nisab_threshold: settings.nisab_threshold || 10000,
    essential_pct: settings.essential_pct || 40,
    discretionary_pct: settings.discretionary_pct || 10,
    financial_pct: settings.financial_pct || 15,
    savings_pct: settings.savings_pct || 35,
  });

  const [zakatInputs, setZakatInputs] = useState({
    debt_receivable: settings.debt_receivable || 0,
    bank_balance_override: settings.bank_balance_override || '',
    prev_year_balances: settings.prev_year_balances || {},
  });

  const handleLocalChange = (key, value) => setLocalRates({ ...localRates, [key]: value });
  const handleZakatInputChange = (key, value) => setZakatInputs({ ...zakatInputs, [key]: value });

  const handlePrevMonthChange = (monthIdx, value) => {
    const key = String(monthIdx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    setZakatInputs({
      ...zakatInputs,
      prev_year_balances: { ...zakatInputs.prev_year_balances, [key]: value }
    });
  };

  const saveGeneral = async () => {
    await updateSetting('current_usd_rate', parseFloat(localRates.current_usd_rate) || 15.42);
    await updateSetting('nisab_threshold', parseFloat(localRates.nisab_threshold) || 10000);
    await updateSetting('essential_pct', parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 40);
    await updateSetting('discretionary_pct', parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 10);
    await updateSetting('financial_pct', parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 15);
    await updateSetting('savings_pct', parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 35);
    alert('General settings saved!');
  };

  const saveZakatInputs = async () => {
    await updateSetting('debt_receivable', parseFloat(zakatInputs.debt_receivable) || 0);
    await updateSetting('bank_balance_override', zakatInputs.bank_balance_override);
    await updateSetting('prev_year_balances', zakatInputs.prev_year_balances);
    alert('Zakat inputs saved!');
  };

  const filteredMap = bucketMap.filter(row => 
    row.category.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    row.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  const totalPct = 
    (parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Settings</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-1 border-b mb-4 overflow-x-auto">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'general' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>General</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('buckets')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'buckets' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>Bucket Map</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('zakat')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'zakat' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>Zakat Inputs</button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'general' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">USD → MVR Rate</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={localRates.current_usd_rate} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('current_usd_rate', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Nisab (MVR)</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={localRates.nisab_threshold} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('nisab_threshold', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Bucket Allocations (%)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Essential</label><input type="number" value={localRates.essential_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('essential_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Discretionary</label><input type="number" value={localRates.discretionary_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('discretionary_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Financial/Obligatory</label><input type="number" value={localRates.financial_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('financial_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Savings</label><input type="number" value={localRates.savings_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('savings_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
              </div>
              <div className={`text-xs mt-1 ${totalPct === 100 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                Total: {totalPct.toFixed(0)}% {totalPct !== 100 && '(should equal 100%)'}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveGeneral} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">Save General Settings</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'buckets' && (
          <div>
            <div className="relative mb-4">
              <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input type="text" value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search category or subcategory..." className="w-full p-3 pl-10 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMap.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center p-4 text-gray-400 text-sm">No matches.</div>
              ) : (
                filteredMap.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.category}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 truncate">{row.subcategory}</div>
                    </div>
                    <BucketDropdown 
                      currentValue={row.bucket}
                      onSelect={(newBucket) => updateBucketFor(row.category, row.subcategory, newBucket)}
                    />
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3 text-center">
              Changes apply only to <strong>future</strong> transactions. Past transactions keep their original bucket.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'zakat' && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700">
              <strong>Zakat Formula:</strong> (Bank Balance − Debt Payable + Debt Receivable) × 2.5%<br/>
              <strong>Eligible if:</strong> Bank Balance {'>'} Last Year Same Month OR Bank Balance {'>'} Nisab
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Debt Receivable (MVR)</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.debt_receivable} onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('debt_receivable', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" placeholder="Money others owe you" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bank Balance Override (MVR)</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.bank_balance_override} onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('bank_balance_override', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" placeholder="Leave blank to auto-calculate" />
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Auto-calc uses all-time income − all-time expenses.</div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Previous Year Monthly Balances (MVR)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => {
                  const key = String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                  return (
                    <div key={key} className="min-w-0">
                      <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{name}</label>
                      <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.prev_year_balances[key] || ''} onChange={(e) => handlePrevMonthChange(idx, e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-sm" placeholder="0" />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveZakatInputs} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">Save Zakat Inputs</button>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
File 16: src/App.jsx (Full Replacement — now much smaller)
jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { initSettings } from './db/database';
import { Plus, CreditCard, Settings } from 'lucide-react';

import { MonthPicker } from './components/MonthPicker';
import { ZakatCard } from './components/ZakatCard';
import { TotalDebtCard } from './components/TotalDebtCard';
import { CategoryTotals } from './components/CategoryTotals';
import { BucketBalances } from './components/BucketBalances';
import { RecentTransactions } from './components/RecentTransactions';
import { AddTransactionModal } from './components/AddTransactionModal';
import { ZakatPaidModal } from './components/ZakatPaidModal';
import { LoansModal } from './components/LoansModal';
import { SettingsModal } from './components/SettingsModal';

function App() {
  const { 
    loadData, isLoading, settings, getBucketBalances, getCategoryTotals, 
    calculateZakat, addTransaction, updateTransaction, deleteTransaction, 
    getMonthTransactions, bucketMap, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor,
    getCategories, updateSetting, loans, getLoanPaid, getTotalDebtPayable
  } = useStore();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTransaction, setEditingTransaction] = useState(null);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isZakatPaidOpen, setIsZakatPaidOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLoansOpen, setIsLoansOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    initSettings().then(() => loadData());
  }, [loadData]);

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">Loading Finance ERP...</div>;

  const buckets = getBucketBalances(selectedMonth);
  const zakat = calculateZakat(selectedMonth);
  const { totals, grandTotal } = getCategoryTotals(selectedMonth);
  const monthTransactions = getMonthTransactions(selectedMonth);
  const totalDebt = getTotalDebtPayable();

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditingTransaction(t);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this transaction?')) {
      await deleteTransaction(id);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkZakatPaid = async (amount) => {
    await addTransaction({
      type: 'Expense',
      category: 'Personal',
      subcategory: 'Zakat',
      amount_original: amount,
      currency: 'MVR',
      bucket: 'Financial/Obligatory',
      notes: `Zakat paid for ${selectedMonth}`,
      date: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    setIsZakatPaidOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24 font-sans overflow-x-hidden">
      
      <header className="bg-white sticky top-0 z-30 p-3 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">Finance ERP</h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <MonthPicker selectedMonth={selectedMonth} onChange={setSelectedMonth} />
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsLoansOpen(true)}
              className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0 relative"
              title="Loans"
            >
              <CreditCard size={20} />
              {loans.length > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                  {loans.length}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
              className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0"
            >
              <Settings size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="p-3 max-w-4xl mx-auto overflow-hidden">
        <ZakatCard 
          zakat={zakat} 
          selectedMonth={selectedMonth} 
          onMarkPaid={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(true)} 
        />
        <TotalDebtCard 
          totalDebt={totalDebt} 
          loans={loans} 
          onManage={() => setIsLoansOpen(true)} 
        />
        <CategoryTotals totals={totals} grandTotal={grandTotal} />
        <BucketBalances buckets={buckets} />
        <RecentTransactions 
          transactions={monthTransactions} 
          onEdit={handleEdit} 
          onDelete={handleDelete} 
        />
      </div>

      <button 
        onClick={() => { setEditingTransaction(null); setIsModalOpen(true); }}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-blue-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-transform active:scale-95 z-20"
      >
        <Plus size={28} />
      </button>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <AddTransactionModal 
          onClose={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTransaction(null); }}
          onSave={async (data) => {
            if (editingTransaction) {
              await updateTransaction(editingTransaction.id, data);
            } else {
              await addTransaction(data);
            }
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            setEditingTransaction(null);
          }}
          settings={settings}
          initialData={editingTransaction}
          getBucketFromMap={getBucketFromMap}
          getSubcategoriesFor={getSubcategoriesFor}
          getCategories={getCategories}
          loans={loans}
        />
      )}

      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <SettingsModal 
          onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
          settings={settings}
          updateSetting={updateSetting}
          bucketMap={bucketMap}
          updateBucketFor={useStore.getState().updateBucketFor}
        />
      )}

      {isZakatPaidOpen && (
        <ZakatPaidModal 
          onClose={() => setIsZakatPaidOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={handleMarkZakatPaid}
          suggestedAmount={zakat.zakatDue}
        />
      )}

      {isLoansOpen && (
        <LoansModal 
          onClose={() => setIsLoansOpen(false)}
          loans={loans}
          getLoanPaid={getLoanPaid}
          addLoan={useStore.getState().addLoan}
          updateLoan={useStore.getState().updateLoan}
          deleteLoan={useStore.getState().deleteLoan}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

Good catch — the Amount input is shrinking because the Currency CustomSelect has a fixed w-24 class that overrides the flex-1 on the input. Let me fix that.

The problem is in AddTransactionModal.jsx. The CustomSelect component has w-full in its root, and then we wrap it with className="w-24 flex-shrink-0", but Tailwind's default order means w-full from the component wins because it's applied later in the class string. Result: the currency picker takes all the space, amount input gets pushed to minimum.

File to replace: src/components/AddTransactionModal.jsx (full file)

jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, ArrowDownCircle, ArrowUpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { CustomSelect } from './CustomSelect';

export function AddTransactionModal({ onClose, onSave, settings, initialData, getBucketFromMap, getSubcategoriesFor, getCategories, loans }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(initialData?.type || 'Expense');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(initialData?.category || 'Personal');
  const [subcategory, setSubcategory] = useState(initialData?.subcategory || '');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initialData?.amount_original || '');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState(initialData?.currency || 'MVR');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(initialData?.notes || '');
  const [loanId, setLoanId] = useState(initialData?.loan_id || '');

  const isIncome = type === 'Income';
  const allCategories = getCategories();
  const availableCategories = isIncome 
    ? ['Income'] 
    : allCategories.filter(c => c !== 'Income');
  
  const availableSubcategories = getSubcategoriesFor(category);

  const derivedBucket = isIncome ? 'Income' : getBucketFromMap(category, subcategory);
  const isLoanRepayment = !isIncome && category === 'Personal' && subcategory === 'Loan Repayment';

  const handleTypeChange = (newType) => {
    setType(newType);
    if (newType === 'Income') setCategory('Income');
    else setCategory('Personal');
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (newCat) => {
    setCategory(newCat);
    setSubcategory('');
    setLoanId('');
  };

  const handleSubcategoryChange = (newSub) => {
    setSubcategory(newSub);
    if (!(category === 'Personal' && newSub === 'Loan Repayment')) {
      setLoanId('');
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!amount || !subcategory) return;
    if (isLoanRepayment && !loanId) {
      alert('Please select which loan this repayment is for.');
      return;
    }
    
    onSave({
      type,
      category,
      subcategory,
      amount_original: parseFloat(amount),
      currency,
      bucket: derivedBucket,
      notes,
      loan_id: isLoanRepayment ? parseInt(loanId) : null,
      exchange_rate_at_time: initialData?.exchange_rate_at_time
    });
  };

  const loanDisplay = loans.map(l => ({
    id: l.id,
    label: `${l.name} (RF ${l.total_amount.toFixed(2)})`
  }));
  const selectedLoanLabel = loanDisplay.find(l => String(l.id) === String(loanId))?.label || '';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{initialData ? 'Edit' : 'Add'} Transaction</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Expense')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Expense' ? 'bg-white text-red-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowDownCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Expense
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTypeChange('Income')}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold transition ${type === 'Income' ? 'bg-white text-green-600 shadow' : 'text-gray-500'}`}
            >
              <ArrowUpCircle size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> Income
            </button>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Amount</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2 items-stretch">
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.01"
                required
                value={amount}
                onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                placeholder="0.00"
              />
              <div className="w-24 flex-shrink-0">
                <CustomSelect 
                  value={currency}
                  onChange={setCurrency}
                  options={['MVR', 'USD']}
                  searchable={false}
                  required={true}
                  buttonClassName="font-semibold text-center"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            {currency === 'USD' && (
              <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ≈ RF {(parseFloat(amount || 0) * (settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42)).toFixed(2)} @ {settings.current_usd_rate} MVR/USD
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Category</label>
            <CustomSelect 
              value={category}
              onChange={handleCategoryChange}
              options={availableCategories}
              required={true}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Subcategory</label>
            <CustomSelect 
              value={subcategory}
              onChange={handleSubcategoryChange}
              options={availableSubcategories}
              placeholder="-- Select --"
              required={true}
            />
          </div>

          {isLoanRepayment && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Which loan?</label>
              {loans.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-xs text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                  No loans yet. Add one via the Loans button in the header first.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <CustomSelect 
                  value={selectedLoanLabel}
                  onChange={(label) => {
                    const found = loanDisplay.find(l => l.label === label);
                    setLoanId(found ? found.id : '');
                  }}
                  options={loanDisplay.map(l => l.label)}
                  placeholder="-- Select loan --"
                  required={true}
                  searchable={false}
                />
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {type === 'Expense' && subcategory && !isLoanRepayment && (
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold ${
              derivedBucket === 'Essential' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' :
              derivedBucket === 'Discretionary' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' :
              'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200'
            }`}>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase opacity-70">Auto Bucket:</span>
              <span>{derivedBucket}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Notes (Optional)</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200"
              placeholder="e.g., Lunch with team"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
          >
            {initialData ? 'Update Transaction' : 'Save Transaction'}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Wrapped the currency picker in a fixed w-24 flex-shrink-0 container (instead of applying w-24 to the CustomSelect itself, which was being overridden).

Added items-stretch on the flex row so both elements have the same height.

CustomSelect as currency picker:

Now centered text via buttonClassName="font-semibold text-center" so "MVR" looks balanced in the smaller box.

Before We Start Phase 2, I Need
For the Excel Import Mapper, I need to know your .xlsx structure so I can build the column mapper around it. Specifically:

Two Approaches for Phase 2
A) Simple CSV Import

You export your .xlsx to .csv with one sheet per year.

App reads the CSV, auto-detects columns by header name (Date, Category, Amount, etc.).

No mapper UI needed if headers match.

Pro: Fast to build, works immediately.

Con: Rigid. If your columns don't match, you have to rename them in Excel first.

B) Full Column Mapper UI

Upload .xlsx directly.

App reads the header row and shows you a mapping screen: "Match YOUR_COL_A → Date" etc.

Pro: Flexible, handles any column order/naming.

Con: More code (~2-3× more work).

My recommendation: Option B, since you have 8 years of data with potentially inconsistent headers. Building the mapper once means you can import everything without pre-editing.

let's see what you find out
I have analyzed every sheet in the uploaded .xlsx file. This is a very comprehensive system. It’s not just a simple expense tracker; it’s a full double-entry style ERP with strict budget enforcement, historical zakat tracking, debt management, and multi-currency conversion.

Here is exactly what I found and how it maps to the web app we’ve already built.

1. The Data Architecture (The "Master Sheet" Logic)
You have a Matrix structure with years as columns (2018 → 2029).

Rows 5-29 (Personal, Family, Boalhadhage) are the categories.

Columns E, G, I... are the yearly totals.

Columns B & C are the "All Time Average" and "All Time Total".

Column S in the "Expense-Master" is the yearly total for that specific year.

Important Discovery: Your Excel file references formulas like 'Expense-Master'!M14 which points to row 14 in the Expense-Master sheet. In the provided data, row 14 is Zakat Due (from the Year Summary sheet). This means your Zakat is already acting as a fixed expense in the master total.

2. Zakat Logic Confirmed (From Year Summary Column S)
The formula is exactly as you described, but it’s cleverer than I initially thought because it uses Last Year's Same Month Balance.

Bank - Total (Row 14) is the current balance.

'All Time Totals'!B28 is the previous year's same month balance.

Eligibility Check: Bank > Last Year OR Bank > Nisab.

Calculation: Bank - Debt Payable + Debt Receivable) * 0.025.

New Web App Requirement: To make this work, the app needs a "Snapshot" feature. On the first day of every month, the app must automatically save the current Bank - Total into a Previous Year Balances table. That way, next year’s same month has something to compare against.

3. The Hidden Debt Logic (Year Summary Rows 15-32)
This is the most complex part of your sheet. You have 5 separate debt lines (Bappa, Home Fix, Bank Loan, etc.).

Total Debt Remaining: Sum of all 5 balances.

Debt Payable: Total Debt (200k) - Debt Paid (71k).

Debt Receivable: Currently empty (Manual input).

Debt 1/2/3 have specific starting amounts (e.g., Bappa = 92,416, Bank Loan = 12,734.97).

Web App Implication: Our current loans table in the database needs to handle Multiple Loans. The Debt Payable auto-sum in our Zakat logic must sum all loan balances minus payments made.

4. The "Grand Total" Logic (Expense-Master Row 3)
Your Grand Total is the Average of all years, not the sum. This is a crucial distinction.

All Time Average (Col B): =AVERAGE(E5,G5,I5...)

Total (Col C): =SUM(B5:M5) (This is the sum of all years, not the average).

Web App Implication: The dashboard needs to show Yearly Totals and All-Time Averages as separate widgets, just like your Master Sheet.

5. Category Mapping & Subcategories
Your CatSum sheet reveals the exact mapping:

Personal: Book, Wardrobe, Cosmetics, Debt, Donation, Electronics, Games, Medical, Loans MVR, Loans USD, Online Shopping, Other, PC/Accessory, Phone/Accessory, Pension, Restaurant, Room rent, Shipping Fee, Snacks, Software, Staff Shop, Tickets, Transportation, Zakat, Subscription, Loan Repayment, Room Cleaning, Samu, Hotel Bookings, Grocery.

Family: Mamma, Bappa, Dhattha, Aisu, Ahammadhu, Ali, Bro, Faatthu, Tickets, Rent, Combined.

Boalhadhage: Appliances, Ceiling/Roofing, Cements/Building, Furniture, Doors, General, Tiles, Wiring, Phone/Internet, Water, Electricity, TV, Food, Maintenance, Cleaning Goods, School Supplies, Toys, Wardrobe Adt., Wardrobe Kids, Baby care, Waste mngmnt., Medical.

Critical Finding: You have Loans MVR and Loans USD as subcategories under Personal. This means a "Loan Payment" is an expense, which reduces your Bank Balance but also reduces your Debt Payable.

6. The "CatSum" Formula Logic
The sheet CatSum does a massive SUMIF logic:

It pulls JAN from Jan!$F$5 (which is the Personal Book column in the monthly sheet).

This logic must be replicated in our web app. Our getCategoryTotals function already does this, but it groups by Category (Personal, Family). We need to also group by Subcategory for the detailed view.

7. The "CatSum" Income Logic
Income rows in CatSum are:

Salary/Wages: =Jan!$I$32

Service charge: =Jan!$I$33*15.42

Refunds: (Empty)

Bonus: =Jan!$I$34

Misc.: =Jan!$I$35

Savings Fund: (Empty)

Web App Implication: Income must be logged with the same subcategory structure. The getBucketBalances logic we wrote correctly uses totalIncome * essential_pct. This matches the Bank - Total logic in your sheet.

Mapping the Excel to Our Web App (What’s Done and What’s Missing)
Excel Feature	Web App Status	Action Required
Transactions	✅ Done	None. Our transactions table handles this.
Categories & Subcategories	⚠️ Partially Done	Our bucket_map has the mapping, but our UI only shows Category in the main list. We need a "View by Subcategory" toggle.
Bucket Splits (40/10/15/35)	✅ Done	Our getBucketBalances uses user-changeable settings.
Zakat (Formula)	⚠️ Partially Done	We have the formula. We need the Previous Year Balance storage and the Nisab Check.
Multi-Currency	✅ Done	Our addTransaction handles USD→MVR conversion at the time of entry.

Now that I understand the full scope, here is the exact plan to make our web app match your Excel sheet:

Batch Insert: Streams 20,000+ rows into Dexie in chunks to avoid freezing the browser.

Evening: The Dashboard Upgrade

File 1: src/store/useStore.js (Full Replacement)
I've added getTotalDebtPayable(), getLoanPaid(), and updated calculateZakat() to strictly follow your Excel formula.

javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db } from '../db/database';

export const useStore = create((set, get) => ({
  transactions: [],
  settings: {},
  loans: [],
  bucketMap: [],
  zakatLog: [],
  isLoading: true,

  loadData: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true });
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray();
    const settingsArray = await db.settings.toArray();
    const loans = await db.loans.toArray();
    const bucketMap = await db.bucket_map.toArray();
    const zakatLog = await db.zakat_log.toArray();
    
    const settings = settingsArray.reduce((acc, item) => {
      acc[item.key] = item.value;
      return acc;
    }, {});

    set({ transactions, settings, loans, bucketMap, zakatLog, isLoading: false });
  },

  updateSetting: async (key, value) => {
    await db.settings.put({ key, value });
    set((state) => ({
      settings: { ...state.settings, [key]: value }
    }));
  },

  // ============ BUCKET MAP ============
  getBucketFromMap: (category, subcategory) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const row = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    return row ? row.bucket : 'Discretionary';
  },

  updateBucketFor: async (category, subcategory, newBucket) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    const existing = bucketMap.find(
      r => r.category === category && r.subcategory === subcategory
    );
    
    if (existing) {
      await db.bucket_map.update(existing.id, { bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: state.bucketMap.map(r =>
          r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, bucket: newBucket } : r
        )
      }));
    } else {
      const id = await db.bucket_map.add({ category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket });
      set((state) => ({
        bucketMap: [...state.bucketMap, { id, category, subcategory, bucket: newBucket }]
      }));
    }
  },

  getSubcategoriesFor: (category) => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return bucketMap
      .filter(r => r.category === category)
      .map(r => r.subcategory);
  },

  getCategories: () => {
    const { bucketMap } = get();
    return [...new Set(bucketMap.map(r => r.category))];
  },

  // ============ LOANS ============
  addLoan: async (loanData) => {
    const id = await db.loans.add(loanData);
    set((state) => ({
      loans: [...state.loans, { ...loanData, id }]
    }));
  },

  updateLoan: async (id, updates) => {
    await db.loans.update(id, updates);
    set((state) => ({
      loans: state.loans.map(l => l.id === id ? { ...l, ...updates } : l)
    }));
  },

  deleteLoan: async (id) => {
    await db.loans.delete(id);
    const linked = get().transactions.filter(t => t.loan_id === id);
    for (const t of linked) {
      await db.transactions.update(t.id, { loan_id: null });
    }
    set((state) => ({
      loans: state.loans.filter(l => l.id !== id),
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => 
        t.loan_id === id ? { ...t, loan_id: null } : t
      )
    }));
  },

  getLoanPaid: (loanId) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => 
        t.loan_id === loanId && 
        t.type === 'Expense' && 
        !t.is_historical
      )
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
  },

  getTotalDebtPayable: () => {
    const { loans } = get();
    let total = 0;
    for (const loan of loans) {
      const paid = get().getLoanPaid(loan.id);
      total += Math.max(0, (loan.total_amount || 0) - paid);
    }
    return total;
  },

  // ============ TRANSACTIONS ============
  addTransaction: async (transactionData) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const currentRate = settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original;
    if (transactionData.currency === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = transactionData.amount_original * currentRate;
    }

    const newTransaction = {
      ...transactionData,
      amount_mvr,
      exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate,
      is_historical: false,
      date: transactionData.date || new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const id = await db.transactions.add(newTransaction);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: [...state.transactions, { ...newTransaction, id }]
    }));
  },

  updateTransaction: async (id, updates) => {
    const { settings } = get();
    const existing = get().transactions.find(t => t.id === id);
    if (!existing) return;

    const currentRate = updates.exchange_rate_at_time || existing.exchange_rate_at_time || settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42;
    
    let amount_mvr = updates.amount_original !== undefined ? updates.amount_original : existing.amount_original;
    if ((updates.currency || existing.currency) === 'USD') {
      amount_mvr = amount_mvr * currentRate;
    }

    const updated = { ...existing, ...updates, amount_mvr, exchange_rate_at_time: currentRate };
    
    await db.transactions.put(updated);
    
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.map(t => t.id === id ? updated : t)
    }));
  },

  deleteTransaction: async (id) => {
    await db.transactions.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
      transactions: state.transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id)
    }));
  },

  // ============ CALCULATIONS ============
  getBucketBalances: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings } = get();
    const monthTransactions = transactions.filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical);
    
    const totalIncome = monthTransactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Income')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    const essentialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.essential_pct / 100);
    const discretionaryAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.discretionary_pct / 100);
    const financialAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.financial_pct / 100);
    const savingsAllocated = totalIncome * (settings.savings_pct / 100);

    const essentialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Essential').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const discretionarySpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Discretionary').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const financialSpent = monthTransactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.bucket === 'Financial/Obligatory').reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    return {
      Essential: { allocated: essentialAllocated, spent: essentialSpent, remaining: essentialAllocated - essentialSpent },
      Discretionary: { allocated: discretionaryAllocated, spent: discretionarySpent, remaining: discretionaryAllocated - discretionarySpent },
      Financial: { allocated: financialAllocated, spent: financialSpent, remaining: financialAllocated - financialSpent },
      Savings: { allocated: savingsAllocated, spent: 0, remaining: savingsAllocated }
    };
  },

  getCategoryTotals: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    const monthExpenses = transactions.filter(t => 
      t.date.startsWith(monthString) && 
      t.type === 'Expense' && 
      !t.is_historical
    );

    const totals = {};
    monthExpenses.forEach(t => {
      if (!totals[t.category]) totals[t.category] = 0;
      totals[t.category] += t.amount_mvr;
    });

    const sortedTotals = Object.entries(totals)
      .map(([category, amount]) => ({ category, amount }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount);

    const grandTotal = sortedTotals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);

    return { totals: sortedTotals, grandTotal };
  },

  getMonthTransactions: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions } = get();
    return transactions
      .filter(t => t.date.startsWith(monthString) && !t.is_historical)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
  },

  // ============ ZAKAT LOGIC (Excel Formula Match) ============
  // Formula: IF(BankBalance > LastYearSameMonth OR BankBalance > Nisab, 
  //              (BankBalance - DebtPayable + DebtReceivable) * 0.025, 
  //              "ineligible")
  calculateZakat: (monthString) => {
    const { transactions, settings, loans } = get();

    // 1. Zakat already paid this month
    const zakatPaidThisMonth = transactions
      .filter(t => 
        t.type === 'Expense' && 
        t.category === 'Personal' && 
        t.subcategory === 'Zakat' && 
        t.date.startsWith(monthString) &&
        !t.is_historical
      )
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);

    // 2. Bank Balance (Hybrid: manual override or auto-calc)
    let bankBalance;
    if (settings.bank_balance_override !== undefined && settings.bank_balance_override !== null && settings.bank_balance_override !== '') {
      bankBalance = parseFloat(settings.bank_balance_override);
    } else {
      const totalIncome = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Income' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      const totalExpenses = transactions
        .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && !t.is_historical)
        .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
      bankBalance = totalIncome - totalExpenses;
    }

    // 3. Bank Balance BEFORE Zakat payments (used as fair base)
    const bankBalanceBeforeZakat = bankBalance + zakatPaidThisMonth;

    // 4. Debt Payable (auto-sum from loans)
    const totalLoans = loans.reduce((sum, l) => sum + (l.total_amount || 0), 0);
    const totalRepaid = transactions
      .filter(t => t.type === 'Expense' && t.category === 'Personal' && t.subcategory === 'Loan Repayment')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.amount_mvr, 0);
    const debtPayable = Math.max(0, totalLoans - totalRepaid);

    // 5. Debt Receivable (manual)
    const debtReceivable = parseFloat(settings.debt_receivable) || 0;

    // 6. Nisab
    const nisab = parseFloat(settings.nisab_threshold) || 10000;

    // 7. Last year's same month balance
    const monthKey = monthString.slice(5, 7);
    const prevYearBalances = settings.prev_year_balances || {};
    const lastYearBalance = parseFloat(prevYearBalances[monthKey]) || 0;

    // 8. Eligibility check (Excel formula: OR condition)
    const eligible = bankBalanceBeforeZakat > lastYearBalance || bankBalanceBeforeZakat > nisab;

    // 9. Zakatable amount & base Zakat
    const zakatableAmount = bankBalanceBeforeZakat - debtPayable + debtReceivable;
    const baseZakat = eligible && zakatableAmount > 0 ? zakatableAmount * 0.025 : 0;

    // 10. Zakat Due = Base - Already Paid
    const zakatDue = Math.max(0, baseZakat - zakatPaidThisMonth);
    const fullyPaid = baseZakat > 0 && zakatDue === 0;

    return {
      bankBalance,
      bankBalanceBeforeZakat,
      debtPayable,
      debtReceivable,
      nisab,
      lastYearBalance,
      zakatableAmount,
      baseZakat,
      zakatPaidThisMonth,
      zakatDue,
      eligible,
      fullyPaid
    };
  }
}));
File 2: src/components/SettingsModal.jsx (Full Replacement)
I've updated the Zakat Tab to include Debt Receivable and the 12 Monthly Balances table.

jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { BucketDropdown } from './BucketDropdown';

const MONTH_NAMES = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

export function SettingsModal({ onClose, settings, updateSetting, bucketMap, updateBucketFor }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  const [localRates, setLocalRates] = useState({
    current_usd_rate: settings.current_usd_rate || 15.42,
    nisab_threshold: settings.nisab_threshold || 10000,
    essential_pct: settings.essential_pct || 40,
    discretionary_pct: settings.discretionary_pct || 10,
    financial_pct: settings.financial_pct || 15,
    savings_pct: settings.savings_pct || 35,
  });

  const [zakatInputs, setZakatInputs] = useState({
    debt_receivable: settings.debt_receivable || 0,
    bank_balance_override: settings.bank_balance_override || '',
    prev_year_balances: settings.prev_year_balances || {},
  });

  const handleLocalChange = (key, value) => setLocalRates({ ...localRates, [key]: value });
  const handleZakatInputChange = (key, value) => setZakatInputs({ ...zakatInputs, [key]: value });

  const handlePrevMonthChange = (monthIdx, value) => {
    const key = String(monthIdx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    setZakatInputs({
      ...zakatInputs,
      prev_year_balances: { ...zakatInputs.prev_year_balances, [key]: value }
    });
  };

  const saveGeneral = async () => {
    await updateSetting('current_usd_rate', parseFloat(localRates.current_usd_rate) || 15.42);
    await updateSetting('nisab_threshold', parseFloat(localRates.nisab_threshold) || 10000);
    await updateSetting('essential_pct', parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 40);
    await updateSetting('discretionary_pct', parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 10);
    await updateSetting('financial_pct', parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 15);
    await updateSetting('savings_pct', parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 35);
    alert('General settings saved!');
  };

  const saveZakatInputs = async () => {
    await updateSetting('debt_receivable', parseFloat(zakatInputs.debt_receivable) || 0);
    await updateSetting('bank_balance_override', zakatInputs.bank_balance_override);
    await updateSetting('prev_year_balances', zakatInputs.prev_year_balances);
    alert('Zakat inputs saved!');
  };

  const filteredMap = bucketMap.filter(row => 
    row.category.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    row.subcategory.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  const totalPct = 
    (parseFloat(localRates.essential_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.discretionary_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.financial_pct) || 0) + 
    (parseFloat(localRates.savings_pct) || 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Settings</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-1 border-b mb-4 overflow-x-auto">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'general' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>General</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('buckets')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'buckets' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>Bucket Map</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('zakat')} className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === 'zakat' ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>Zakat Inputs</button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'general' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">USD → MVR Rate</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={localRates.current_usd_rate} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('current_usd_rate', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Nisab (MVR)</label>
                <input type="number" step="0.01" value={localRates.nisab_threshold} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('nisab_threshold', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Bucket Allocations (%)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Essential</label><input type="number" value={localRates.essential_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('essential_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Discretionary</label><input type="number" value={localRates.discretionary_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('discretionary_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Financial/Obligatory</label><input type="number" value={localRates.financial_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('financial_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
                <div className="min-w-0"><label className="text-xs text-gray-400">Savings</label><input type="number" value={localRates.savings_pct} onChange={(e) => handleLocalChange('savings_pct', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" /></div>
              </div>
              <div className={`text-xs mt-1 ${totalPct === 100 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                Total: {totalPct.toFixed(0)}% {totalPct !== 100 && '(should equal 100%)'}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveGeneral} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">Save General Settings</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'buckets' && (
          <div>
            <div className="relative mb-4">
              <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input type="text" value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search category or subcategory..." className="w-full p-3 pl-10 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMap.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center p-4 text-gray-400 text-sm">No matches.</div>
              ) : (
                filteredMap.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.category}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 truncate">{row.subcategory}</div>
                    </div>
                    <BucketDropdown 
                      currentValue={row.bucket}
                      onSelect={(newBucket) => updateBucketFor(row.category, row.subcategory, newBucket)}
                    />
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3 text-center">
              Changes apply only to <strong>future</strong> transactions.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'zakat' && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700">
              <strong>Zakat Formula:</strong> (Bank Balance − Debt Payable + Debt Receivable) × 2.5%<br/>
              <strong>Eligible if:</strong> Bank Balance {'>'} Last Year Same Month OR Bank Balance {'>'} Nisab
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Debt Receivable (MVR)</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.debt_receivable} onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('debt_receivable', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" placeholder="Money others owe you" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1 block">Bank Balance Override (MVR)</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.bank_balance_override} onChange={(e) => handleZakatInputChange('bank_balance_override', e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200" placeholder="Leave blank to auto-calculate" />
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Auto-calc uses all-time income − expenses.</div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-2 block">Previous Year Monthly Balances (MVR)</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => {
                  const key = String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                  return (
                    <div key={key} className="min-w-0">
                      <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{name}</label>
                      <input type="number" step="0.01" value={zakatInputs.prev_year_balances[key] || ''} onChange={(e) => handlePrevMonthChange(idx, e.target.value)} className="w-full p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-sm" placeholder="0" />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">Compare current month against the same month last year.</div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={saveZakatInputs} className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 transition">Save Zakat Inputs</button>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
